Public Class frmAddInventory

    Public Enum UserAccess
        ''' <summary>
        ''' User that can add inventory without a PO or restrictions
        ''' </summary>
        FULL_ACCESS
        ''' <summary>
        ''' User that can add inventory with an existing PO for the selected part
        ''' </summary>
        PO_MANDATORY
        ''' <summary>
        ''' User that can add inventory with a recieved PO for the selected part
        ''' </summary>
        RECIEVED_PO_MANDATORY
        ''' <summary>
        ''' User that can saved inventory with reasons diffrents than PURCADD
        ''' </summary>
        GENERAL_USER
        ''' <summary>
        ''' User that does not have access to save inventory
        ''' </summary>
        NO_ACCESS
    End Enum

    Public ReadOnly Property IsDisassemblyPart() As Boolean
        Get
            If (ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders Is Nothing) Then
                Throw New Exception("Unable to load the Infragistics package. =(")
            End If
            If (ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow IsNot Nothing) Then
                If (ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow.HasParent()) Then
                    Dim PoLine = TryCast(ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow.ParentRow.ListObject, QuickReportSchema)
                    Dim child = TryCast(ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow.ListObject, DisassemblyPart)
                    If (PoLine.PartNo = child.ChildPartNo) Then
                        Return False
                    End If
                    Return True
                End If
            End If
            Return False
        End Get
    End Property


    Public ReadOnly Property IsSelfContainedDisassembly() As Boolean
        Get
            If (ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders Is Nothing) Then
                Throw New Exception("Unable to load the Infragistics package. =(")
            End If
            If (ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow IsNot Nothing AndAlso ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow.HasParent()) Then
                Dim PoLine = TryCast(ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow.ParentRow.ListObject, QuickReportSchema)
                Dim child = PoLine.Disassemblies.FirstOrDefault(Function(item) item.ChildPartNo = PoLine.PartNo)
                Return child IsNot Nothing
            End If

            Return False
        End Get
    End Property

    Public ReadOnly Property CurrentFinesseQty() As Integer
        Get
            If (_selected_ifs_po IsNot Nothing) Then
                Dim partno = _selected_ifs_po.PartNo

                Dim result = 0 '_selected_ifs_po.FinesseQty

                'Dim ActualFinesseQty = 0
                Dim inventoryQty = (New ReceivedInventory()).GetReceivedInventory(partno, _selected_ifs_po.OrderNo, _selected_ifs_po.LineNo.ToString, _selected_ifs_po.ReleaseNo.ToString).GetAwaiter().GetResult()
                result += Integer.Parse(txtQty.Text) + inventoryQty.onhand
                'If (Not IsDisassemblyPart) Then
                '    result += Integer.Parse(txtQty.Text) + inventoryQty.onhand
                'Else
                '    'Dim row = ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow.ParentRow
                '    'partno = row.Cells("ChildPartNo").Value.ToString()
                '    'Dim POElements = DataSetAddInventory.inpartsub.Where(Function(item) item.PONumber = _selected_ifs_po.OrderNo AndAlso item.partno = partno AndAlso item.POReleaseNo = partno)
                '    'If (POElements IsNot Nothing AndAlso POElements.Count > 0) Then
                '    '    result += POElements.Sum(Function(item) item.onhand)
                '    'End If
                '    result = 0
                'End If

                Return result
            End If

            Return dgvItemsForImport.Rows.Count
        End Get
    End Property

    Public ReadOnly Property IsValidEntry() As (Boolean, String)
        Get
            Dim isValid = True
            Dim msg As String

            Dim po = _selected_ifs_po
            Dim selectedRow
            If (ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow IsNot Nothing) Then
                selectedRow = TryCast(ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow.ListObject, QuickReportSchema)
            End If


            If (po Is Nothing AndAlso selectedRow IsNot Nothing) Then
                po = selectedRow
            End If

            If (cboRef4.SelectedValue = "PURCADD" And Not IsDisassemblyPart) Then
                If (_selected_ifs_po Is Nothing) Then
                    _selected_ifs_po = po
                End If

                If ((po Is Nothing OrElse ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow Is Nothing) OrElse po.PartNo <> txtPartNo.Text OrElse po.OrderNo <> txtPONumber.Text) Then
                    isValid = False
                    msg = "Is Mandatory to select a PO Line in order to add items to inventory."
                    'ElseIf (po IsNot Nothing AndAlso CurrentFinesseQty > po.InvQtyArrived) Then
                    '    isValid = False
                    '    msg = "Overstocking inventory is not allowed. The quantity added to the inventory cannot exceed the quantity specified in the Purchase Order."
                ElseIf (po IsNot Nothing AndAlso Not po.IsValidSchema) Then
                    isValid = False
                    msg = po.SchemaError
                End If
            End If
            If (_userPermission = UserAccess.FULL_ACCESS AndAlso Not isValid) Then
                Dim result = MessageBox.Show($"The supplied PO information is not valid to be Proccessed. {vbNewLine}This won't send a succesfull message to IFS.{vbNewLine}Are you sure you want to continue?", "Info", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.Information)
                If (result = DialogResult.Yes) Then
                    Return (True, "FULL_ACCESS")
                End If
            End If

            Return (isValid, msg)
        End Get
    End Property

    Public Property UserPermission() As UserAccess
        Get
            Return _userPermission
        End Get
        Set(value As UserAccess)
            _userPermission = value
        End Set
    End Property

#End Region

#Region "Functions"

    Public Function IsFormClosing() As Boolean
        Dim stackTrace As StackTrace = New StackTrace
        For Each sf As StackFrame In stackTrace.GetFrames
            If (sf.GetMethod.Name = WMCLOSE) Then Return True
        Next
        Return False
    End Function

    Private Function GetUserWarehouse()
        Using newConn As New SqlConnection(FinesseConnectionString)
            newConn.Open()
            GetUserWarehouse = newConn.ExecuteScalar("select warehouse_code from warehouses where is_default = 1")
        End Using
    End Function

    Private Function HasDataChanged() As Boolean

        For Each r As DataRow In DataSetAddInventory.inpartsub
            Select Case r.RowState
                Case DataRowState.Unchanged
                    'Nothing
                Case Else
                    Return True
            End Select
        Next

        Return False
    End Function

#End Region

    End Sub

    Private Sub EnableDoubleBuffering(ByVal control As Control)
        GetType(DataGridView).InvokeMember(
                    "DoubleBuffered",
                    Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic Or Reflection.BindingFlags.Instance Or Reflection.BindingFlags.SetProperty,
                    Nothing,
                    control,
                    New Object() {True}
                    )
    End Sub

    Private Sub frmAddInventory_KeyDown(sender As Object, e As KeyEventArgs) Handles Me.KeyDown
        If e.KeyCode = Keys.F5 Then RefreshCurrentInventory()
    End Sub

    Private Sub InitializeMenuStripMessage()
        Dim panel As New FlowLayoutPanel()
        panel.FlowDirection = FlowDirection.LeftToRight
        panel.AutoSize = True
        panel.WrapContents = False
        panel.Margin = Padding.Empty
        panel.Padding = Padding.Empty

        panel.BackColor = UltraGridMenuStrip.BackColor

        AddHandler panel.Click, AddressOf menuItemSendMessage_Click

        Dim newLabel As New Label()
        newLabel.Text = "Send Msg w/Qty:"
        newLabel.AutoSize = True
        newLabel.TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleLeft
        newLabel.Margin = New Padding(0, 3, 5, 0)
        newLabel.BackColor = UltraGridMenuStrip.BackColor

        AddHandler newLabel.Click, AddressOf menuItemSendMessage_Click

        Dim textBox As New TextBox()
        textBox.Width = 100
        txtQuantityMessage = textBox
        panel.Controls.Add(newLabel)
        panel.Controls.Add(txtQuantityMessage)


        Dim tsHost As New ToolStripControlHost(panel)
        tsHost.Margin = Padding.Empty
        menuItemSendMessage = tsHost


        UltraGridMenuStrip.Items.Add(menuItemSendMessage)
    End Sub

    Private Sub textBox_KeyPress(sender As Object, e As KeyPressEventArgs) Handles txtQuantityMessage.KeyPress
        If Char.IsControl(e.KeyChar) Then
            Return
        End If

        If Not Char.IsDigit(e.KeyChar) Then
            e.Handled = True
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Sub textBox_KeyDown(sender As Object, e As KeyEventArgs) Handles txtQuantityMessage.KeyDown
        If (e.KeyCode = Keys.Enter) Then
            SendIFSToFinesseMessage()
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Sub SendIFSToFinesseMessage()
        If clickedRow IsNot Nothing Then
            Dim row = TryCast(clickedRow.ListObject, QuickReportSchema)

            Dim qty = 0
            Integer.TryParse(txtQuantityMessage.Text, qty)
            If (String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtQuantityMessage.Text) OrElse qty = 0) Then
                Dim toast As New ToastNotification($"You need to specify a valid quantity!", Color.Red)
                toast.Show()
                Exit Sub
            End If

            Dim result = row.SendMessage(_dtCurrency, txtQuantityMessage.Text)
            If (Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(result)) Then
                Dim toast As New ToastNotification($"Message successfully Sent! [{result}]", Color.Green)
                toast.Show()
            Else
                Dim toast As New ToastNotification($"Error sending the message!", Color.Red)
                toast.Show()
            End If
            UltraGridMenuStrip.Close()
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Sub menuItemSendMessage_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs)
        SendIFSToFinesseMessage()
    End Sub

    Private Sub frmAddInventory_Load(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As EventArgs) Handles MyBase.Load
        DgvInventoryInStock.AutoGenerateColumns = False
        Try
            Dim bandChild = ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.DisplayLayout.Bands(1)
            bandChild.Override.AllowColSizing = Infragistics.Win.UltraWinGrid.AllowColSizing.Free
        Catch ex As Exception

        End Try

        InitializeMenuStripMessage()
        'DgvERPPurchaseOrders.VirtualMode = True
        Dim args As ObjectModel.ReadOnlyCollection(Of String) = My.Application.CommandLineArgs
        Dim argSerialNo As String = Nothing
        Dim argPartNo As String = Nothing

        If args.Count > 0 Then
            For Each arg In args
                If InStr(arg.ToString(), "serialno=") Then argSerialNo = Replace(arg.ToString(), "serialno=", "")
                If InStr(arg.ToString(), "serialno:") Then argSerialNo = Replace(arg.ToString(), "serialno:", "")
                If InStr(arg.ToString(), "partno=") Then argPartNo = Replace(arg.ToString(), "partno=", "")
                If InStr(arg.ToString(), "partno:") Then argPartNo = Replace(arg.ToString(), "partno:", "")
            Next
        End If

        lblCurrentInventoryWarning.Dock = DockStyle.Fill

        EnableDoubleBuffering(dgvCurrentInventory)

        Dim jammer As New SQLJammer(New SqlConnection(FinesseConnectionString))

        jammer.Add("select * from App_Config c where c.cmndsel = " & cmndsel.SQLQuote,
                   Sub(t)
                       AppConfig = t
                       'zebraPrinterConfig = New ZebraPrinterConfig(AppConfig)
                   End Sub)

        jammer.Add("select 
                        can_edit_inventory = dbo.can_edit_inventory(),
                        can_add_inventory_without_restrictions = dbo.can_add_inventory_without_restrictions(),
                        can_add_inventory_with_unrecieved_purchase_order = dbo.can_add_inventory_with_unrecieved_purchase_order(),
                        can_add_inventory_only_recieved_purchase_order = dbo.can_add_inventory_only_recieved_purchase_order(),
                        can_add_edit_disassemblies_subparts = dbo.can_add_edit_disassemblies_subparts(),
                        owner_entity, warehouse_entity, currency, id_language, unitofweight = iuow.conversionLongname, unitoflength = iuol.conversionLongname, BarcodeSequenceType = isnull(p.BarcodeSequenceType, ''), p.user_name from dbo.pjtfrusr p join dbo.inUnitOfWeight iuow on p.unitofweight = iuow.conversionShortname join dbo.inUnitOfLength iuol on p.unitoflength = iuol.conversionShortname where p.user_name = suser_sname()",
                   Sub(t)
                       Dim row = t.Rows(0)
                       _can_edit_inventory = row.Item("can_edit_inventory")
                       Dim _can_edit_inventory_no_restrictions = row.Item("can_add_inventory_without_restrictions")
                       Dim _can_edit_inventory_with_po = row.Item("can_add_inventory_with_unrecieved_purchase_order")
                       Dim _can_edit_inventory_with_recieved_po = row.Item("can_add_inventory_only_recieved_purchase_order")
                       _can_add_edit_disassemblies_subparts = row.Item("can_add_edit_disassemblies_subparts")
                       Dim _can_edit_inventory_no_po = row.Item("can_edit_inventory")

                       If (_can_edit_inventory_no_restrictions) Then
                           UserPermission = UserAccess.FULL_ACCESS
                       ElseIf (_can_edit_inventory_with_po) Then
                           UserPermission = UserAccess.PO_MANDATORY
                       ElseIf (_can_edit_inventory_with_recieved_po) Then
                           UserPermission = UserAccess.RECIEVED_PO_MANDATORY
                       ElseIf (_can_edit_inventory_no_po) Then
                           UserPermission = UserAccess.GENERAL_USER
                       Else
                           UserPermission = UserAccess.NO_ACCESS
                       End If


                       MasterDataToolStripMenuItem.Visible = _can_add_edit_disassemblies_subparts
                       USER_DEFAULT_COMPANY = row.Item("owner_entity")
                       USER_DEFAULT_WAREHOUSE = row.Item("warehouse_entity")
                       USER_DEFAULT_CURRENCY = row.Item("currency")
                       USER_DEFAULT_ID_LANGUAGE = row.Item("id_language")
                       ReloadPermissions()
                   End Sub)

        jammer.Add("select eo.OwnerCode, eo.OwnerDesc, EnableInpartsubRefChildLinking = isnull(c.EnableInpartsubRefChildLinking , 0), 
                                EnableZebraPrinting = isnull(eo.EnableZebraPrinting, 0), eo.BarcodeSequenceType from EquipmentOwner eo 
                       left join dbo.Company c on c.CompanyCode = eo.CompanyCode where eo.AllowAddNewEquipment = 1",
                   Sub(t)
                       _dtOwners = t
                       Init_OwnersCombo()
                       Init_ERP_OwnersCombo()
                   End Sub)
        jammer.Add("select currency, description, symbol, CapexThreshold from dbo.Currency",
                   Sub(t)
                       _dtCurrency = t
                   End Sub)

        'jammer.Add("select warehouse_code = WarehouseCode , warehouse_description = w.WarehouseDesc from dbo.warehouse w",
        jammer.Add("select DISTINCT warehouse_code = WarehouseCode , warehouse_description = w.WarehouseDesc, IFSCompanyCd = ISNULL(c.IFSCompanyCd, WarehouseCode) from dbo.warehouse w
                          LEFT JOIN dbo.Company c ON w.CompanyCode = c.CompanyCode
                          WHERE w.WarehouseCode IN (select warehouse_code from dbo.warehouses)
                    ORDER BY WarehouseCode",
                   Sub(t)
                       _dtWarehouses = t
                       Init_WarehousesCombo()
                       Init_ERP_WarehousesCombo()
                   End Sub)

        jammer.Add("select ref4, ref5desc, i.description from dbo.intransref4 i union all select ref4='', ref5desc='', description = '(none)' order by description",
                   Sub(t)
                       _dtReasonRef4 = t
                       Init_Ref4Combo()
                   End Sub)

        jammer.Execute()

        Init_dgcs()


        'Dim foundRows = From row In _dtOwners.AsEnumerable()
        '                Where row.Field(Of String)("OwnerCode") = USER_DEFAULT_COMPANY
        'If (Not foundRows.Any) Then
        '    cboOwner.SelectedValue = USER_DEFAULT_COMPANY
        'End If 

        Try
            cboOwner.SelectedValue = USER_DEFAULT_COMPANY
            cboWarehouse.SelectedValue = USER_DEFAULT_WAREHOUSE
            cboErpWarehouse.SelectedValue = USER_DEFAULT_WAREHOUSE
            cboErpOwner.SelectedValue = USER_DEFAULT_COMPANY
        Catch ex As Exception

        End Try


        IsDataLoaded = True


        FillERPGrid()

        txtPartNo.Text = argPartNo

        hideOrDisplayAutoIPAddressForPart(argPartNo)

        Select Case IIf(Get_AppConfigSetting(AppConfig, "MainFormWindowState") IsNot Nothing, Get_AppConfigSetting(AppConfig, "MainFormWindowState"), Me.WindowState)
            Case FormWindowState.Maximized.ToString
                Dim MainFormLocation = Get_AppConfigSetting(AppConfig, "MainFormLocation")
                If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(MainFormLocation) Then Me.DesktopLocation = Get_LocationFromString(MainFormLocation)
                Me.WindowState = FormWindowState.Maximized
            Case FormWindowState.Normal.ToString
                Me.WindowState = FormWindowState.Normal
                Dim MainFormLocation = Get_AppConfigSetting(AppConfig, "MainFormLocation")
                If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(MainFormLocation) Then Me.DesktopLocation = Get_LocationFromString(MainFormLocation)
                Dim MainFormSize = Get_AppConfigSetting(AppConfig, "MainFormSize")
                If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(MainFormSize) Then Me.Size = Get_SizeFromString(MainFormSize)
        End Select


        Dim lastOwner = Get_AppConfigSetting(AppConfig, "LastOwnerCode")
        If lastOwner IsNot Nothing Then
            Dim exists = _dtOwners.Select($"OwnerCode = '{lastOwner.ToString}'")
            If exists.Count > 0 Then
                cboOwner.SelectedValue = lastOwner
                UpdatePrintBehavior()
            End If
        End If

        Dim lastWarehouse = Get_AppConfigSetting(AppConfig, "LastSelectedWarehouse")
        If lastWarehouse IsNot Nothing Then
            Dim exists = _dtWarehouses.Select($"warehouse_code = '{lastWarehouse.ToString}'")
            If exists.Count > 0 Then
                cboWarehouse.SelectedValue = lastWarehouse
            End If
        End If

        ShowButtonTooltip()
        HandlePartImageGUI()

        Dim activateZebraPrint As Boolean = EnabledZebraPrinting(cboOwner.SelectedValue)
        btnGetNextBarcode.Visible = activateZebraPrint
    End Sub

    Private Sub ShowButtonTooltip()
        If (btnAddItemToList.Enabled) Then
            btnAddItemToList.Enabled = Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtPartNo.Text)
        End If
    End Sub
    Private Sub ReloadPermissions()
        If (UserPermission = UserAccess.FULL_ACCESS OrElse
                    (UserPermission <> UserAccess.NO_ACCESS AndAlso cboRef4.SelectedValue <> "PURCADD") OrElse
                    (UserPermission = UserAccess.PO_MANDATORY AndAlso _selected_ifs_po IsNot Nothing) OrElse
                    (UserPermission = UserAccess.RECIEVED_PO_MANDATORY AndAlso _selected_ifs_po IsNot Nothing AndAlso _selected_ifs_po.IsRecieved)) Then
            SaveToolStripMenuItem.Enabled = True
            btnCommit.Enabled = True
            btnAddItemToList.Enabled = True
            txtPONumber.Enabled = cboRef4.SelectedValue = "MANFADD" OrElse (UserPermission = UserAccess.FULL_ACCESS)
            LblPermissionMsg.Text = ""
            If (cboRef4.SelectedValue <> "PURCADD" OrElse (_selected_ifs_po IsNot Nothing AndAlso txtPartNo.Text <> _selected_ifs_po.PartNo)) Then
                _selected_ifs_po = Nothing
                txtPONumber.Text = ""
                txtQty.Text = ""
            ElseIf (_selected_ifs_po IsNot Nothing) Then
                txtPONumber.Text = _selected_ifs_po.OrderNo
            End If
        Else
            SaveToolStripMenuItem.Enabled = False
            btnCommit.Enabled = False
            btnAddItemToList.Enabled = False
            txtPONumber.Enabled = False

            If (cboRef4.SelectedValue = "PURCADD") Then
                If ((_selected_ifs_po Is Nothing OrElse ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow Is Nothing) OrElse _selected_ifs_po IsNot Nothing AndAlso _selected_ifs_po.PartNo <> txtPartNo.Text) Then
                    LblPermissionMsg.Text = "You need to select a PO"
                Else

                    If ({UserAccess.NO_ACCESS, UserAccess.GENERAL_USER}.Contains(UserPermission) OrElse
                    UserPermission <> UserAccess.FULL_ACCESS AndAlso _selected_ifs_po Is Nothing OrElse
                    UserPermission = UserAccess.RECIEVED_PO_MANDATORY AndAlso _selected_ifs_po IsNot Nothing AndAlso Not _selected_ifs_po.IsRecieved) Then
                        LblPermissionMsg.Text = "You don't have permission to perform this operation"

                    End If
                End If
            End If

            '    If ((cboRef4.SelectedValue = "PURCADD" AndAlso Not {UserAccess.NO_ACCESS, UserAccess.GENERAL_USER}.Contains(UserPermission) AndAlso Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtPartNo.Text)) _
            '        AndAlso (UserPermission = UserAccess.RECIEVED_PO_MANDATORY AndAlso _selected_ifs_po IsNot Nothing AndAlso _selected_ifs_po.IsRecieved) _
            '        OrElse _selected_ifs_po IsNot Nothing AndAlso _selected_ifs_po.PartNo <> txtPartNo.Text) Then
            '    LblPermissionMsg.Text = "You need to select a PO"
            'Else
            '    LblPermissionMsg.Text = "You don't have permission to perform this operation"
            'End If

            LblPermissionMsg.Location = New Point(btnAddItemToList.Location.X - LblPermissionMsg.Width - 10, btnAddItemToList.Location.Y)
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Sub btnAddItemToList_Hover(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As EventArgs) Handles btnAddItemToList.MouseHover
        ToolTip1.Show(If(btnAddItemToList.Enabled, "Add item to list", "You must input a part number"), btnAddItemToList)
    End Sub

    Private Sub btnAddItemToList_UnHover(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As EventArgs) Handles btnAddItemToList.MouseDown
        ToolTip1.Hide(btnAddItemToList)
    End Sub

    Private Sub frmAddInventory_FormClosing(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As FormClosingEventArgs) Handles Me.FormClosing
        Dim sw As New Stopwatch
        sw.Start()

        Debug.Print(sw.ElapsedMilliseconds & " Before DoYouWantToSave")

        Dim lShouldReturn As Boolean
        DoYouWantToSave(lShouldReturn)
        If lShouldReturn Then Return

        Debug.Print(sw.ElapsedMilliseconds & " After DoYouWantToSave")

        Try
            Debug.Print(sw.ElapsedMilliseconds & " Before OpenConnection")

            Dim newConn As New SqlConnection(FinesseConnectionString)
            newConn.Open()

            Debug.Print(sw.ElapsedMilliseconds & " After OpenConnection")

            Dim SettingsToSave As New List(Of AppConfigState) From {
                New AppConfigState() With {.Cmndsel = cmndsel, .Tag = "MainFormSize", .Rvalue = Me.Size.ToString},
                New AppConfigState() With {.Cmndsel = cmndsel, .Tag = "MainFormLocation", .Rvalue = Me.Location.ToString},
                New AppConfigState() With {.Cmndsel = cmndsel, .Tag = "MainFormWindowState", .Rvalue = Me.WindowState.ToString}
            }

            If (Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(cboOwner.SelectedValue)) Then
                SettingsToSave.Add(New AppConfigState() With {.Cmndsel = cmndsel, .Tag = "LastOwnerCode", .Rvalue = cboOwner.SelectedValue})
            End If

            If (Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(cboWarehouse.SelectedValue)) Then
                SettingsToSave.Add(New AppConfigState() With {.Cmndsel = cmndsel, .Tag = "LastSelectedWarehouse", .Rvalue = cboWarehouse.SelectedValue})
            End If

            Debug.Print(sw.ElapsedMilliseconds & " After array of settings")

            SettingsToSave.SaveConfig()

            newConn.Close()
            newConn.Dispose()

            Debug.Print(sw.ElapsedMilliseconds & " After Close Connection")

        Catch ex As Exception
            Console.WriteLine("Error saving App Configs: ")
            Console.WriteLine(ex.Message)
        End Try

    End Sub

#End Region

#Region "Initializations"

    Private Sub Init_OwnersCombo()
        cboOwner.DisplayMember = "OwnerDesc"
        cboOwner.ValueMember = "OwnerCode"
        cboOwner.DataSource = New DataView(_dtOwners, "", "OwnerDesc asc", DataViewRowState.CurrentRows)
    End Sub
    Private Sub Init_ERP_OwnersCombo()
        cboErpOwner.DisplayMember = "OwnerDesc"
        cboErpOwner.ValueMember = "OwnerCode"
        Dim table = _dtOwners.Copy()
        Dim newRow As DataRow = table.NewRow()
        newRow("OwnerCode") = ""
        newRow("OwnerDesc") = "(All Owners)"
        newRow("EnableInpartsubRefChildLinking") = False
        newRow("EnableZebraPrinting") = False
        newRow("BarcodeSequenceType") = ""
        table.Rows.InsertAt(newRow, 0)

        cboErpOwner.DataSource = New DataView(table, "", "OwnerDesc asc", DataViewRowState.CurrentRows)
    End Sub

    Private Sub Init_WarehousesCombo()
        cboWarehouse.DisplayMember = "warehouse_description"
        cboWarehouse.ValueMember = "warehouse_code"
        cboWarehouse.DataSource = New DataView(_dtWarehouses, "", "warehouse_description asc", DataViewRowState.CurrentRows)
    End Sub

    Private Sub Init_ERP_WarehousesCombo()
        cboErpWarehouse.DisplayMember = "warehouse_description"
        cboErpWarehouse.ValueMember = "warehouse_code"
        Dim table = _dtWarehouses.Copy()
        Dim newRow As DataRow = table.NewRow()
        newRow("warehouse_code") = ""
        newRow("warehouse_description") = "(All warehouses)"
        table.Rows.InsertAt(newRow, 0)
        cboErpWarehouse.DataSource = New DataView(table, "", "warehouse_description asc", DataViewRowState.CurrentRows)
    End Sub

    Private Sub Init_Ref4Combo()
        cboRef4.DisplayMember = "description"
        cboRef4.ValueMember = "ref4"
        cboRef4.DataSource = New DataView(_dtReasonRef4, "ref4 not in ('REVCHG','WHCHG','SCRAP','LOST','SALE','OWNERCHG','REPAIR','SN Chg','MAINT')", "description asc", DataViewRowState.CurrentRows)
    End Sub

    Private Sub Init_dgcs()
        dgcAddItemsDept.DisplayMember = cboOwner.DisplayMember
        dgcAddItemsDept.ValueMember = cboOwner.ValueMember
        dgcAddItemsDept.DataSource = cboOwner.DataSource

        dgcAddItemsBld.DisplayMember = cboWarehouse.DisplayMember
        dgcAddItemsBld.ValueMember = cboWarehouse.ValueMember
        dgcAddItemsBld.DataSource = cboWarehouse.DataSource

        dgcAddItemRef4.DisplayMember = cboRef4.DisplayMember
        dgcAddItemRef4.ValueMember = cboRef4.ValueMember
        dgcAddItemRef4.DataSource = cboRef4.DataSource
    End Sub

#Region "File Menu"

    Private Sub SaveToolStripMenuItem_Click(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As EventArgs) Handles SaveToolStripMenuItem.Click, btnCommit.Click
        If dgvItemsForImport.Rows.Count = 0 Then
            Beep_Fail()
            MsgBox("You do not have any items in your list to add into inventory." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & "Commit has been aborted.", vbOKOnly)
            Return
        Else
            'If (Not IsValidEntry) Then
            '    Beep_Fail()
            '    MsgBox("Overstocking inventory is not allowed. The quantity added to the inventory cannot exceed the quantity specified in the Purchase Order." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & "Commit has been aborted.", vbOKOnly)
            '    Return
            'End If
            Dim uniqueRows = DataSetAddInventory.inpartsub.AsEnumerable() _
                                .GroupBy(Function(row) New With {
                                    Key .PartNo = row.Field(Of String)("partno"),
                                    Key .PONumber = row.Field(Of String)("PONumber"),
                                    Key .POLineNo = row.Field(Of String)("POLineNo"),
                                    Key .POReleaseNo = row.Field(Of String)("POReleaseNo")
                                }) _
                                .Select(Function(g) g.First()) _
                                .CopyToDataTable()
            SaveData()
            ReloadSavedPOs(uniqueRows)

            RefreshCurrentInventory()
            ItemsForImportQtyUpdating()
        End If
    End Sub


    Private Function SaveData()
        If Not Validate() Then Return False

        bsAddInventory.EndEdit()

        If ValidateAddToInventoryItems() = False Then Return False

        SaveData = False
        Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor

        Try
            Dim sSQL As New StringBuilder

            sSQL.AppendLine("delete inpsAddInventory where session_id = @@spid;")
            gConn.ExecuteNonQuery(sSQL)

            'Dim uniqueRows = DataSetAddInventory.inpartsub.AsEnumerable() _
            '                    .GroupBy(Function(row) New With {
            '                        Key .PartNo = row.Field(Of String)("partno"),
            '                        Key .OrderNo = row.Field(Of String)("orderno")
            '                    }) _
            '                    .Select(Function(g) g.First()) _
            '                    .CopyToDataTable()



            BulkSave(DataSetAddInventory.inpartsub, "inpsAddInventory")
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand("exec commit_add_inventory", gConn)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            cmd.CommandTimeout = 120
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            For Each rv As DataGridViewRow In dgvItemsForImport.Rows
                If rv.Cells(RemoveNonCoded.Index).EditedFormattedValue = True And Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(rv.Cells(dgcAddItemsBarcode.Index).Value) Then
                    Dim sSQL2 As New StringBuilder
                    sSQL2.AppendLine("if exists(select * from dbo.inpartsub ps where ps.partno = " & SQLQuote(rv.Cells(PartnoDataGridViewTextBoxColumn.Index).Value) & " and ps.bld = " & SQLQuote(rv.Cells(dgcAddItemsBld.Index).Value) & " and ps.dept = " & SQLQuote(rv.Cells(dgcAddItemsDept.Index).Value) & " and ps.batchno = 'INSTOCK' and ps.unique_no = '' and ps.parentunique_no = '' and onhand > 0)")
                    sSQL2.AppendLine("begin")
                    sSQL2.AppendLine("  insert dbo.intrans")
                    sSQL2.AppendLine("        ( trantype , partno ,unique_no, onhand ,qty ,trandate ,ref4 ,ref5 ,batchno ,dept ,bld ,row ,serial_no ,enterdate, userid  )")
                    sSQL2.AppendLine("  select top 1 'F', " & SQLQuote(rv.Cells(PartnoDataGridViewTextBoxColumn.Index).Value) & ",'', ps.onhand, -1, cast(floor(cast(getdate() as float)) as datetime), 'NONTOBAR', " & SQLQuote(rv.Cells(dgcAddItemsBarcode.Index).Value) & ",'INSTOCK', ps.dept, ps.bld, ps.row, ps.serial_no, getdate(), suser_sname()")
                    sSQL2.AppendLine("  from dbo.inpartsub ps")
                    sSQL2.AppendLine("  where ps.partno = " & SQLQuote(rv.Cells(PartnoDataGridViewTextBoxColumn.Index).Value) & " and ps.bld = " & SQLQuote(rv.Cells(dgcAddItemsBld.Index).Value) & " and ps.dept = " & SQLQuote(rv.Cells(dgcAddItemsDept.Index).Value) & " and ps.batchno = 'INSTOCK' and ps.unique_no = '' and ps.parentunique_no = '' and onhand > 0")
                    sSQL2.AppendLine(" ")
                    sSQL2.AppendLine("  update top (1) ps set onhand = onhand - 1")
                    sSQL2.AppendLine("  from dbo.inpartsub ps ")
                    sSQL2.AppendLine("  where ps.partno = " & SQLQuote(rv.Cells(PartnoDataGridViewTextBoxColumn.Index).Value) & " and ps.bld = " & SQLQuote(rv.Cells(dgcAddItemsBld.Index).Value) & " and ps.dept = " & SQLQuote(rv.Cells(dgcAddItemsDept.Index).Value) & " and ps.batchno = 'INSTOCK' and ps.unique_no = '' and ps.parentunique_no = '' and onhand > 0")
                    sSQL2.AppendLine("end")
                    gConn.ExecuteNonQuery(sSQL2)
                End If
            Next

            DataSetAddInventory.inpartsub.AcceptChanges()
            SaveData = True

            'MsgBox("Inventory added successfully.", vbOKOnly)
            Dim toast As New ToastNotification($"Inventory added successfully.", Color.Green)
            toast.Show()
            DataSetAddInventory.inpartsub.Clear()
            'Return uniqueRows
        Catch ex As Exception
            Beep_Fail()
            MsgBox("Error while saving: " & ex.Message, MsgBoxStyle.Exclamation)
        End Try

        Cursor = Cursors.Default
    End Function

    Private Sub RefreshCurrentInventory()
        Dim currentTotal As String
        Dim partno As String = txtPartNo.Text
        Dim owner As String = cboOwner.SelectedValue
        Dim warehouse As String = cboWarehouse.SelectedValue

        If String.IsNullOrEmpty(partno) OrElse String.IsNullOrEmpty(owner) OrElse String.IsNullOrEmpty(warehouse) Then
            lblCurrentInventoryWarning.Visible = True
            Exit Sub
        Else
            lblCurrentInventoryWarning.Visible = False
        End If

        Dim sSQL As New StringBuilder
        sSQL.AppendLine("select Qty = ps.onhand, Barcode = ps.unique_no, [Phase No] = ps.batchno, [Phase Desc] = g.entitydesc, WH = ps.bld, [Owner] = ps.dept, [Return] = pj.todate, ps.receipt")
        sSQL.AppendLine("from dbo.inpartsub ps")
        sSQL.AppendLine("join dbo.glentities g on ps.batchno = g.entityno")
        sSQL.AppendLine("left outer join dbo.pjjobbudexp pj on g.entityno = pj.entityno and ps.partno = pj.partno")
        sSQL.AppendLine("where ps.partno = " & partno.SQLQuote)
        sSQL.AppendLine("and ps.dept = " & owner.SQLQuote)
        sSQL.AppendLine("and ps.bld = " & warehouse.SQLQuote)
        sSQL.AppendLine("and ps.onhand > 0")
        sSQL.AppendLine("order by ps.receipt DESC, ps.unique_no")

        Using newConn = New SqlConnection(FinesseConnectionString)
            Dim t = newConn.GetDataTable(sSQL)
            dgvCurrentInventory.DataSource = t
            currentTotal = IIf(String.IsNullOrEmpty(t.Compute("Sum(qty)", "").ToString), "0", t.Compute("Sum(qty)", "").ToString)
            newConn.Close()
        End Using

        tsslCurrentInventoryTotalQty.Text = $"Total Qty: {currentTotal}"
        ItemsForImportQtyUpdating()
    End Sub

    Private Sub BulkSave(ByVal tSource As DataTable, ByVal destinationTable As String)
        Dim tTemp As New DataTable

        tTemp.Columns.Add("is_insert", GetType(Boolean)).DefaultValue = False
        tTemp.Columns.Add("is_update", GetType(Boolean)).DefaultValue = False
        tTemp.Columns.Add("is_delete", GetType(Boolean)).DefaultValue = False

        For Each c As DataColumn In tSource.Columns
            If Not c.ReadOnly Then tTemp.Columns.Add(c.ColumnName, c.DataType) ' assuming that read-only columns will by calculated automatically by the DB
        Next

        For Each r As DataRow In tSource.Rows
            Select Case r.RowState
                Case DataRowState.Added, DataRowState.Deleted, DataRowState.Modified
                    tTemp.ImportRow(r)
                Case Else
                    Debug.Assert(r.RowState = DataRowState.Unchanged)
            End Select
        Next

        For Each r As DataRow In tTemp.Rows
            Select Case r.RowState
                Case DataRowState.Added
                    r("is_insert") = True
                Case DataRowState.Modified
                    r("is_update") = True
                Case DataRowState.Deleted
                    r.RejectChanges() ' necessary so that we can look at and modify fields.
                    r("is_delete") = True
            End Select
        Next

        Using bulkCopy As New SqlBulkCopy(gConn)
            bulkCopy.DestinationTableName = destinationTable
            bulkCopy.BatchSize = 0
            bulkCopy.ColumnMappings.Clear()

            For Each c As DataColumn In tTemp.Columns
                Debug.Print(c.ColumnName)
                bulkCopy.ColumnMappings.Add(c.ColumnName, c.ColumnName)
            Next

            bulkCopy.WriteToServer(tTemp)
        End Using
    End Sub

    Private Sub ExitToolStripMenuItem_Click(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As EventArgs) Handles ExitToolStripMenuItem.Click
        Dim lShouldReturn As Boolean
        DoYouWantToSave(lShouldReturn)
        If lShouldReturn Then Return
        End
    End Sub

#Region "Adding Items to List"

    Private Async Sub btnAddItemToList_Click(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As EventArgs) Handles btnAddItemToList.Click
        Dim Validation = IsValidEntry
        If (Not Validation.Item1) Then
            MsgBox(Validation.Item2, MsgBoxStyle.Critical)
            Return
        End If

        If (String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtSerialNo.Text) And String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtBarcode.Text) And String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtQty.Text)) Then
            txtBarcode.Focus()
            Return
        End If

        If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtSerialNo.Text) And String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtBarcode.Text) Then
            txtBarcode.Focus()
            Return
        End If

        Dim qty As Integer

        Try
            qty = CInt(txtQty.Text)
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("Quantity must be a whole number greater than zero.", vbOKOnly)
            Return
        End Try

        If CInt(txtQty.Text) < 1 Then
            MsgBox("Quantity must be a whole number greater than zero.", vbOKOnly)
            Return
        End If

        Dim lShouldReturn As Boolean
        ValidateNewInventoryItemInfo(lShouldReturn)
        If lShouldReturn Then Return

        'Set ShouldLinkChildParts to false as the default before any "add item" processing begins.
        Dim ShouldLinkChildParts As Boolean = False
        Dim dtLinkedParts As New DataTable

        Try
            Dim childPartLinkingAllowed As Boolean = DirectCast(cboOwner.SelectedItem, DataRowView)("EnableInpartsubRefChildLinking")

            If Not childPartLinkingAllowed Then GoTo StagePartForAdd


            Using conn As New SqlConnection(FinesseConnectionString), cmd As New SqlCommand("GetLinkedChildPartInformation", conn), ad As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)
                conn.Open()
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.StoredProcedure
                With cmd.Parameters
                    ''Set the size of VarChar max to -1
                    .Add("@partno", SqlDbType.VarChar, 50).Value = txtPartNo.Text
                    .Add("@qty", SqlDbType.Int).Value = qty
                    .Add("@bld", SqlDbType.VarChar, 10).Value = cboWarehouse.SelectedValue
                    .Add("@owner", SqlDbType.VarChar, 10).Value = cboOwner.SelectedValue
                End With
                ad.Fill(dtLinkedParts)
            End Using


            'Child Part Count
            If dtLinkedParts IsNot Nothing AndAlso dtLinkedParts.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Dim msg As New StringBuilder
                msg.AppendLine("Parent part " & txtPartNo.Text & ": " & txtPartDescEngOp.Text & " has linked parts associated with it.  By adding this part, you will be removing linked parts from working inventory:")
                msg.AppendLine("") 'Add an empty space between the message and the parts
                For Each r In dtLinkedParts.Rows
                    msg.AppendLine(r("childPartQtyToLink") & " of " & r("childPartno") & ": " & r("childPartDesc"))
                Next
                msg.AppendLine("") 'Add an empty space between the parts and the working inventory question.
                msg.AppendLine("Do you want to remove linked parts from working inventory?")


                Dim result = MsgBox(msg.ToString, MsgBoxStyle.YesNoCancel, "Linked Parts")

                Select Case result
                    Case MsgBoxResult.Yes
                        Dim childPartsGoingNegativeQty = From r In dtLinkedParts Where (r("childOnhand") - r("childPartQtyToLink")) < 0
                        Dim errExists As Boolean = False
                        For Each r In childPartsGoingNegativeQty
                            'errMsg.AppendLine("There is not enough [Part Type] [Part Description] in [Warehouse] owned by [Owner] to be linked to [Parent Part Description].  The [Parent Part Description] will not be added to inventory.")
                            Dim errMsg = "There are not enough " & r("childPartno") & ": " & r("childPartDesc") & " in " & cboWarehouse.Text & " owned by " & cboOwner.Text & " to be linked to " & txtPartDescEngOp.Text & ".  " & r("childPartQtyToLink") & " " & IsAre(r("childPartQtyToLink")) & " required to be linked to this part." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & "The " & txtPartDescEngOp.Text & " will not be added to inventory."
                            MsgBox(errMsg.ToString,, "Not Enough Linked Child Parts")
                            errExists = True
                        Next
                        If errExists Then Exit Sub

                        ShouldLinkChildParts = True
                    Case MsgBoxResult.No
                        ShouldLinkChildParts = False
                    Case MsgBoxResult.Cancel
                        'Quit, do nothing
                        Exit Sub
                End Select
            End If

            Dim IsValidPO As Boolean = cboRef4.SelectedValue = "PURCADD" AndAlso _selected_ifs_po IsNot Nothing AndAlso _selected_ifs_po.PartNo = txtPartNo.Text AndAlso _selected_ifs_po.OrderNo = txtPONumber.Text

StagePartForAdd:
            If (IsValidPO) Then
                Dim FinesseInventory = Await (New ReceivedInventory).GetReceivedInventory(_selected_ifs_po.PartNo, _selected_ifs_po.OrderNo, _selected_ifs_po.LineNo.ToString, _selected_ifs_po.ReleaseNo.ToString)
                If (FinesseInventory IsNot Nothing) Then
                    Dim maxqty = _selected_ifs_po.InvQtyArrived

                    If (IsDisassemblyPart) Then
                        Dim parentExpectedqty = TryCast(ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow.ParentRow.ListObject, QuickReportSchema)
                        maxqty = maxqty * parentExpectedqty.InvQtyArrived
                    End If

                    Dim t = From r As DataGridViewRow In dgvItemsForImport.Rows
                            Where r.DataBoundItem("partno") = _selected_ifs_po.PartNo AndAlso r.DataBoundItem("PONumber") = _selected_ifs_po.OrderNo
                            Select total = r.DataBoundItem("onhand")

                    Dim totalItemsAddedQty = t.Sum(Function(r) r)
                    Dim onhand = FinesseInventory.onhand + CInt(txtQty.Text) + totalItemsAddedQty
                    If (onhand > maxqty) Then
                        If (UserPermission = UserAccess.FULL_ACCESS) Then
                            Dim result = MessageBox.Show($"Overstocking inventory shouldn't be possible. The quantity added to the inventory should not exceed the quantity specified in the Purchase Order.{vbNewLine}Max quantity: [{maxqty}] Current Inventory [{FinesseInventory.onhand}].{vbNewLine}Adding: [{txtQty.Text}].{vbNewLine}Are you sure you want to continue?", "Info", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.Information)
                            If (result <> DialogResult.Yes) Then
                                Return
                            End If
                        Else
                            MsgBox($"Overstocking inventory is not allowed. The quantity added to the inventory cannot exceed the quantity specified in the Purchase Order.{vbNewLine}Max quantity: [{maxqty}] Current Inventory [{FinesseInventory.onhand}].{vbNewLine}Adding: [{txtQty.Text}].", MsgBoxStyle.Critical)
                            Return
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
                'Else
                '    If (cboRef4.SelectedValue = "PURCADD") Then
                '        MsgBox("This won't send messages to IFS. Make sure you're doing the right thing!")

                '        If (UserPermission <> UserAccess.FULL_ACCESS) Then
                '            Return
                '        End If
                '    End If
            End If


            AddItemRow(ShouldLinkChildParts, Validation)

            txtSerialNo.Clear()
            txtBarcode.Clear()
            txtRevision.Clear()
            txtMACAddress.Clear()


            If chkDeferEnteringMacAddress.Checked Then
                txtSerialNo.Focus()
            Else
                txtMACAddress.Focus()
            End If


            txtQty.Text = 0

        Catch ex As Exception
            Beep_Fail()
            MsgBox(ex.ToString)
        End Try
    End Sub

    Private Sub AddItemRow(ByVal ShouldLinkChildParts As Boolean, ByVal POValidation As (Boolean, String))
        Dim ipAddress = Nothing

        If chkAutoAssignIPAddress.Checked Then
            ipAddress = getNextAvailableIPAddress(txtPartNo.Text)
        End If


        Dim batchno As String
        Dim internalCustomerResult As (String, String) 'Result, Error
        Dim ActiveRow = ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow

        Dim isIFSPOLine = _selected_ifs_po IsNot Nothing AndAlso ActiveRow IsNot Nothing AndAlso cboRef4.SelectedValue = "PURCADD" AndAlso _selected_ifs_po.PartNo = txtPartNo.Text AndAlso _selected_ifs_po.OrderNo = txtPONumber.Text


        Dim _partno = txtPartNo.Text
        Dim _partdesc = txtPartDescEngOp.Text
        Dim _poPartNo = _partno

        Dim parentPartNo As String

        Dim POToWorkWith As QuickReportSchema = _selected_ifs_po
        Dim parent = Nothing

        If (ActiveRow IsNot Nothing AndAlso ActiveRow.HasParent) Then
            parent = TryCast(ActiveRow.ParentRow.ListObject, QuickReportSchema)
        End If

        If (IsDisassemblyPart) Then
            parentPartNo = parent.PartNo
            _poPartNo = parent.PartNo
            If Not IsSelfContainedDisassembly Then
                POToWorkWith = parent
                _poPartNo = parent.PartNo
            End If
        End If

        If (Not IsDisassemblyPart AndAlso parent IsNot Nothing) Then
            POToWorkWith = parent
            _poPartNo = parent.PartNo
        End If


        If (isIFSPOLine AndAlso (Not IsDisassemblyPart Or Not IsSelfContainedDisassembly)) Then
            batchno = POToWorkWith.GetBatchNo(txtSerialNo.Text)
            internalCustomerResult = POToWorkWith.GetInternalCustomer(_dtCurrency)
            Console.WriteLine($"Line Internal Customer: {internalCustomerResult.Item1}")
            If (Not IsDisassemblyPart) Then
                If (internalCustomerResult.Item1 Is Nothing) Then
                    MsgBox(internalCustomerResult.Item2, MsgBoxStyle.Exclamation)
                End If
                If (Not POToWorkWith.IsValidSchema) Then
                    MsgBox(POToWorkWith.SchemaError, MsgBoxStyle.Exclamation)
                End If
                If (String.IsNullOrEmpty(batchno) AndAlso POToWorkWith.IsSerializedPo) Then
                    MsgBox("The supplied serial number does not exists on the IFS Inventory.", MsgBoxStyle.Exclamation)
                End If
                If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(POValidation.Item2) AndAlso POValidation.Item2 <> "FULL_ACCESS" Then
                    Exit Sub
                End If
            End If
        End If


        Dim r = DataSetAddInventory.inpartsub.AddinpartsubRow(partno:=_partno,
                                                          partdesc:=_partdesc,
                                                          unique_no:=txtBarcode.Text,
                                                          batchno:="INSTOCK",
                                                          dept:=cboOwner.SelectedValue,
                                                          bld:=cboWarehouse.SelectedValue,
                                                          row:=txtRevision.Text,
                                                          PONumber:=txtPONumber.Text,
                                                          serial_no:=txtSerialNo.Text,
                                                          receipt:=DateTime.Now,
                                                          onhand:=IIf(String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtQty.Text), "0", txtQty.Text),
                                                          acctno:=String.Empty, ' this field is obsolete
                                                          ref4:=cboRef4.SelectedValue,
                                                          ref5:=txtRef5.Text,
                                                          ShouldLinkChildParts:=ShouldLinkChildParts,
                                                          RemoveNonCoded:=IIf(cboRef4.SelectedValue = "NONTOBAR", True, False),
                                                          IPAddress:=ipAddress,
                                                          MACAddress:=txtMACAddress.Text,
                                                          PODateEntered:=If(isIFSPOLine, POToWorkWith.DateEntered, DateTime.Now),
                                                          POSite:=If(isIFSPOLine, POToWorkWith.Contract, Nothing),
                                                          POInternalCustomer:=If(isIFSPOLine, internalCustomerResult.Item1, Nothing),
                                                          POInternalDestination:=If(isIFSPOLine, POToWorkWith.InternalDestination, Nothing),
                                                          POLineNo:=If(isIFSPOLine, POToWorkWith.LineNo, Nothing),
                                                          POReleaseNo:=If(isIFSPOLine, POToWorkWith.ReleaseNo, Nothing),
                                                          POLocationNo:=If(isIFSPOLine, POToWorkWith.LocationNo, Nothing),
                                                          POLotBatchNo:=If(isIFSPOLine, batchno, Nothing),
                                                          PONoteText:=If(isIFSPOLine, POToWorkWith.NoteText, Nothing),
                                                          POInternalDestinationId:=If(isIFSPOLine, POToWorkWith.DestinationId, Nothing),
                                                          POIsSerialized:=If(isIFSPOLine, POToWorkWith.IsSerializedPo, Nothing),
                                                          IsDisassembly:=If(isIFSPOLine, ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow.HasParent, False),
                                                          ParentPartNo:=If(isIFSPOLine, parentPartNo, Nothing),
                                                          POPartNo:=If(isIFSPOLine, _poPartNo, Nothing),
                                                          POQtyArrived:=If(isIFSPOLine AndAlso parent IsNot Nothing, parent.InvQtyArrived.ToString(), Nothing),
                                                          POBuyUnitPrice:=If(isIFSPOLine, POToWorkWith.BuyUnitPrice, Nothing)
                                                           )

        r.EndEdit()

    End Sub


    Private Sub hideOrDisplayAutoIPAddressForPart(partno As String)

        Dim controls = {Label5, txtSerialNo, pb2Dbarcode, lblBarcode, txtBarcode, btnGetNextBarcode, btnPrintSingleBarcode, lblQty, txtQty}

        If checkIfPartHasIPRangeSet(partno) Then

            If Not txtMACAddress.Visible Then
                For Each c As Control In controls
                    c.Top = c.Top + txtMACAddress.Height
                Next
            End If



            chkAutoAssignIPAddress.Checked = True
            chkAutoAssignIPAddress.Visible = True
            lblMACAddress.Visible = True
            txtMACAddress.Visible = True
            chkDeferEnteringMacAddress.Visible = True
            chkDeferEnteringMacAddress.Checked = False


        Else

            If txtMACAddress.Visible Then
                For Each c As Control In controls
                    c.Top = c.Top - txtMACAddress.Height
                Next
            End If

            chkAutoAssignIPAddress.Checked = False
            chkAutoAssignIPAddress.Visible = False
            lblMACAddress.Visible = False
            txtMACAddress.Visible = False
            txtMACAddress.Text = String.Empty

            'force focus to the serial number after entering an item
            chkDeferEnteringMacAddress.Checked = True
            chkDeferEnteringMacAddress.Visible = False

        End If


    End Sub

    Private Function checkIfPartHasIPRangeSet(partno As String) As Boolean
        Dim conn As SqlConnection = GetOpenedFinesseConnection()
        Dim isRangeSet As Boolean

        isRangeSet = conn.ExecuteScalar($"SELECT CASE WHEN ip.RangeType IS NULL THEN 0
                                          	   ELSE 1 END as isSet
                                          FROM dbo.inpart ip
                                          WHERE ip.partno = '{partno}'")
        conn.Close()
        conn.Dispose()

        Return isRangeSet
    End Function

    Private Function getNextAvailableIPAddress(partno As String) As String

        Dim IPAddress As String

        Dim sqlComm As SqlCommand = New SqlCommand("dbo.Get_next_available_IPAddress", gConn)
        sqlComm.CommandType = CommandType.StoredProcedure
        sqlComm.Parameters.Add("@partno", SqlDbType.VarChar, 50).Value = txtPartNo.Text
        sqlComm.Parameters.Add("@nextIPAddress", SqlDbType.VarChar, 15)
        sqlComm.Parameters("@nextIPAddress").Direction = ParameterDirection.Output
        sqlComm.ExecuteNonQuery()
        IPAddress = sqlComm.Parameters.Item("@nextIPAddress").Value

        Return IPAddress
    End Function


    Private Sub ValidateNewInventoryItemInfo(ByRef shouldReturn As Boolean)
        shouldReturn = True

        If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtSerialNo.Text) And String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtBarcode.Text) Then
            Beep_Fail()
            MsgBox("You have entered a serial number, but no barcode.", vbOKOnly)
            txtBarcode.Focus()
            Return
        End If

        If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtBarcode.Text) And String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtSerialNo.Text) Then
            Beep_Fail()
            MsgBox("You have entered a barcode, but no serial number.", vbOKOnly)
            txtSerialNo.Focus()
            Return
        End If

        If cboRef4.SelectedIndex = 0 Then
            Beep_Fail()
            MsgBox("Please select a reason for adding inventory.", vbOKOnly)
            Return
        End If

        If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(_dtReasonRef4.Select("ref4=" & SQLQuote(cboRef4.SelectedValue))(0)("ref5desc")) Then
            If String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtRef5.Text) Then
                Beep_Fail()
                MsgBox("Please enter a note for reason for adding inventory: " & cboRef4.Text, vbOKOnly)
                Return
            End If
        End If

        If cboRef4.SelectedValue = "NONTOBAR" And String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtBarcode.Text) Then
            Beep_Fail()
            MsgBox("You have selected '" & _dtReasonRef4.Select("ref4='NONTOBAR'")(0)("description") & "' as the reason for adding inventory, but no barcode has been entered.", vbOKOnly)
            Return
        End If

        Dim partno = txtPartNo.Text
        Dim existsInList = DataSetAddInventory.inpartsub.Select("partno=" & SQLQuote(partno) &
                                             " and serial_no=" & SQLQuote(txtSerialNo.Text) &
                                             " and unique_no=" & SQLQuote(txtBarcode.Text) &
                                             " and dept=" & SQLQuote(cboOwner.SelectedValue) &
                                             " and bld=" & SQLQuote(cboWarehouse.SelectedValue) &
                                             " and row=" & SQLQuote(txtRevision.Text))
        '" and PONumber=" & SQLQuote(txtPONumber.Text) & TODO: Update the constraint to include the PO Number to allow same information but Diffrent PO Number
        If existsInList.Length > 0 Then
            Beep_Fail()
            MsgBox("You've already entered this part (" & existsInList(0).Item("partno") & ": " & existsInList(0).Item("partdesc") & ") with the same PO Number, owner, warehouse and revision.  You can make changes to the list below if necessary.", vbOKOnly)
            Return
        End If

        Dim barcodeExistsInList = DataSetAddInventory.inpartsub.Select("unique_no=" & SQLQuote(txtBarcode.Text))
        If barcodeExistsInList.Length > 0 AndAlso Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(barcodeExistsInList(0).Item("unique_no")) Then
            Beep_Fail()
            MsgBox("You've already entered this barcode (" & barcodeExistsInList(0).Item("unique_no") & ").", vbOKOnly)
            Return
        End If

        Dim serial_noExistsInList = DataSetAddInventory.inpartsub.Select("serial_no=" & SQLQuote(txtSerialNo.Text))
        If serial_noExistsInList.Length > 0 AndAlso Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(serial_noExistsInList(0).Item("serial_no")) Then
            Beep_Fail()
            MsgBox("You've already entered this serial number (" & serial_noExistsInList(0).Item("serial_no") & ").", vbOKOnly)
            Return
        End If

        shouldReturn = False
    End Sub

#End Region

#Region "Barcode Printing"

    Private Function PreparePrintDocument() As PrintDocument
        ' Make the PrintDocument object.
        Dim print_document As New PrintDocument

        ' Install the PrintPage event handler.
        AddHandler print_document.PrintPage, AddressOf _
            PrintDocument1_PrintPage

        ' Return the object.
        Return print_document
    End Function

    Private Sub PrintDocument1_PrintPage(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As PrintPageEventArgs) Handles PrintDocument1.PrintPage

        Dim Margin As Integer = 50
        Dim paddingInfoBox As Integer = 25
        Dim paddingText As Integer = 5
        Dim paddingSmallText As Integer = 2
        Dim r_uHeight = 25
        Dim r_uWidth = r_uHeight * (19 / 1.75)
        Dim Point As New Point
        Point.X = 0
        Point.Y = 0

        Dim bcWidth2D As Integer = pb2Dbarcode.Image.Width * 0.5
        Dim bcHeight2D As Integer = pb2Dbarcode.Image.Height * 0.5

        Dim font2Dbarcode As New Font(FontFamily.GenericSansSerif, 5)

        Dim font1Dbarcode As New Font("IDAutomationHC39M", 10)
        'Dim bcWidth1D As Integer = e.Graphics.MeasureString(lblBarcodePrint.Text, font1Dbarcode).Width
        'Dim bcHeight1D As Integer = e.Graphics.MeasureString(lblBarcodePrint.Text, font1Dbarcode).Height

        For i = 0 To 5
            e.Graphics.DrawImage(pb2Dbarcode.Image, Margin + (bcWidth2D * 2) * i, Margin, bcWidth2D, bcHeight2D)
            e.Graphics.DrawString(txtBarcode.Text, font2Dbarcode, Brushes.Black, Margin + (bcWidth2D * 2) * i + (bcWidth2D - e.Graphics.MeasureString(txtBarcode.Text, font2Dbarcode).Width) * 0.5, Margin + bcHeight2D + 2)
            'e.Graphics.DrawString(lblBarcodePrint.Text, font1Dbarcode, Brushes.Black, Margin + (bcWidth1D + 20) * i, Margin + bcHeight2D * 2)
        Next

    End Sub

    Private Sub PrintBarcodes(Optional ItemsToPrint As List(Of ItemsToPrintRow) = Nothing)
        Dim owner = _dtOwners.Select($"OwnerCode = '{cboOwner.SelectedValue}'").FirstOrDefault

        Dim useZebraPrint As Boolean = owner.Item("EnableZebraPrinting")

        If Not useZebraPrint Then 'Special EDS Zebra printing
            Try
                If pb2Dbarcode.Image IsNot Nothing Then
                    'Use default print behavior
                    PrintDialog1.Document = PreparePrintDocument()
                    PrintDialog1.Document.DefaultPageSettings.Landscape = True
                    Dim results = PrintDialog1.ShowDialog()
                    If results = DialogResult.OK Then
                        PrintDocument1.PrinterSettings = PrintDialog1.PrinterSettings
                        'PrintDocument1.Print()
                    End If
                Else
                    MsgBox("Nothing to print.")
                End If
            Catch ex As Exception
                MsgBox("Print Failed: " & ex.Message)
            End Try
        Else
            'Use PrintZPL Zebra print dialog
            ' Try
            If ItemsToPrint.Count = 0 Then
                MsgBox("There is nothing to print in the Items For Import grid.")
                Exit Sub
            End If

            Dim printZPLDialog = New frmPrintZPLDialog(cmndsel)
            printZPLDialog.dtItemsToPrint = ItemsToPrint
            printZPLDialog.StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterParent

            printZPLDialog.ShowDialog()

            'get dialog state for reuse on next print
            _PrintDialogState = printZPLDialog.PrintDialogState

            printZPLDialog.Dispose()
            'Catch ex As Exception
            'MsgBox("Zebra Print Failed: " & ex.Message)
            'End Try
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Sub btnPrintSingleBarcode_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles btnPrintSingleBarcode.Click, PrintToolStripMenuItem.Click
        Dim cantValidate As Boolean
        ValidateNewInventoryItemInfo(cantValidate)

        If Not cantValidate Then
            If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(Trim(txtBarcode.Text)) Then
                Dim dtPrintItems As New List(Of ItemsToPrintRow)
                Dim ipAddress As String = ""
                If chkAutoAssignIPAddress.Checked And txtMACAddress.Visible Then
                    ipAddress = getNextAvailableIPAddress(txtPartNo.Text)
                    ipAddress = If(ipAddress Is Nothing Or IsDBNull(ipAddress), "", ipAddress)
                End If

                dtPrintItems.Add(New ItemsToPrintRow(txtSerialNo.Text, txtBarcode.Text, "", ipAddress, txtMACAddress.Text))
                PrintBarcodes(dtPrintItems)
            Else
                MsgBox("There is nothing to print in the New Inventory Item Barcode field.")
            End If
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Sub tsmPrintBarcodes_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles tsmPrintBarcodes.Click
        Dim itemsToImport = bsAddInventory.GetDataRows(Of inpartsubRow)()
        Dim dtPrintItems As New List(Of ItemsToPrintRow)

        For Each item In itemsToImport
            dtPrintItems.Add(New ItemsToPrintRow(item.serial_no.ToString, item.unique_no.ToString))
        Next

        PrintBarcodes(dtPrintItems)
    End Sub

    Private Sub PrintBArcodesSelectedItemsToolStripMenuItem_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles PrintBArcodesSelectedItemsToolStripMenuItem.Click
        Dim itemsToImport = dgvItemsForImport.SelectedRows
        If (itemsToImport Is Nothing Or itemsToImport?.Count = 0) Then
            MessageBox.Show("There is not selected items to print.")
            Exit Sub
        End If
        'bsAddInventory.GetDataRows(Of inpartsubRow)()
        Dim dtPrintItems As New List(Of ItemsToPrintRow)

        For Each item In itemsToImport
            Dim uniqueId = item.Cells("dgcAddItemsBarcode").Value.ToString
            Dim serialNo = item.Cells("dgcAddItemsSerialNo").Value.ToString
            Dim owner = item.Cells("dgcAddItemsDept").Value.ToString

            dtPrintItems.Add(New ItemsToPrintRow(serialNo, uniqueId))
        Next

        PrintBarcodes(dtPrintItems)
    End Sub

    Private Sub tsmPrintSelectedItemsForImport_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles tsmPrintSelectedItemsForImport.Click
        Dim selectedItemsToImport = dgvItemsForImport.SelectedRows
        Dim dtPrintItems As New List(Of ItemsToPrintRow)

        For Each item As DataGridViewRow In selectedItemsToImport
            dtPrintItems.Add(New ItemsToPrintRow(item.Cells("dgcAddItemsSerialNo").Value.ToString, item.Cells("dgcAddItemsBarcode").Value.ToString))
        Next

        PrintBarcodes(dtPrintItems)
    End Sub

        End Try
    End Sub

#Region "General"

    Private Function DoYouWantToSave(ByRef shouldReturn As Boolean)
        shouldReturn = False
        If HasDataChanged() Then
            Dim result = MsgBox("Do you want to save/commit your changes?", vbYesNoCancel)
            Select Case result
                Case DialogResult.Yes
                    Try
                        DataSetAddInventory.AcceptChanges()
                        SaveData()
                        'Continue closing
                    Catch ex As Exception
                        MsgBox(ex.Message)
                    End Try
                Case DialogResult.No
                    'DataSetAddInventory.AcceptChanges() ' why was this here?
                    'Do Nothing and close
                Case DialogResult.Cancel
                    shouldReturn = True
            End Select
        End If
        Return shouldReturn
    End Function

    Private Sub bsAddInventory_BindingComplete(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As BindingCompleteEventArgs) Handles bsAddInventory.BindingComplete
        If bsAddInventory.Current("ref4") = "NONTOBAR" And Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(bsAddInventory.Current("unique_no")) And bsAddInventory.Current("RemoveNonCoded") = False Then
            Dim result = MsgBox("You have changed your reason for adding inventory to '" & _dtReasonRef4.Select("ref4='NONTOBAR'")(0)("description") & "'.  Would you like to attempt to remove a related non-barcoded item from " & bsAddInventory.Current("bld") & " INSTOCK (Owner: " & bsAddInventory.Current("dept") & "?", vbYesNo)
            If result = MsgBoxResult.Yes Then
                bsAddInventory.Current("RemoveNonCoded") = True
            End If
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Sub bsAddInventory_CurrentChanged(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As EventArgs) Handles bsAddInventory.CurrentChanged
        If bsAddInventory.Current Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
        If bsAddInventory.Current("partno") Is DBNull.Value Then Exit Sub
        Selected_PartNo = bsAddInventory.Current("partno")
        Selected_Barcode = bsAddInventory.Current("unique_no")
        Selected_Warehouse = bsAddInventory.Current("bld")

    End Sub

    Private Sub btnPartNoSearch_Click(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As EventArgs) Handles btnPartNoSearch.Click
        Static picker As New PartPicker(Me, btnPartNoSearch, FinesseConnectionString)
        picker.SearchForBarcode = False

        If Not picker.GetPart() Then
            Exit Sub
        End If

        txtPartNo.Text = picker.PartNumber
        txtPartDescEngOp.Text = picker.PartDesc
        txtPartNo_Validating(Nothing, Nothing)

        hideOrDisplayAutoIPAddressForPart(picker.PartNumber)

    End Sub

    Private Sub txtPartNo_Validating(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As System.ComponentModel.CancelEventArgs) Handles txtPartNo.Validating
        If txtPartNo.ReadOnly Then Exit Sub
        If PartValidated Then Exit Sub

        'Check to see if part number exists in ClairTour.
        Using newConn As New SqlConnection(FinesseConnectionString)
            newConn.Open()

            Dim ClairTourPartNo = newConn.GetDataTable("select partdesc from dbo.inpart p where partno = " & SQLQuote(txtPartNo.Text))

            Dim result_ClairTour As DialogResult = Nothing
            If ClairTourPartNo.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                Beep_Fail()
                MsgBox("This part number does not exist in the rental inventory database.", vbOKOnly)
                txtPartNo.Clear()
                Return
            End If
        End Using
        PartValidated = True
    End Sub
