    Private Sub txtPartNo_TextChanged(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As EventArgs) Handles txtPartNo.TextChanged
        If String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtPartNo.Text) Then
            txtPartDescEngOp.Clear()

        Else

            ShowButtonTooltip()
            Using newConn As New SqlConnection(FinesseConnectionString)
                newConn.Open()

                Dim t = newConn.GetDataTable("select p.partdesc, p.lead_source from inpart p where p.partno = " & SQLQuote(txtPartNo.Text))
                If t.Rows.Count = 0 Then
                    txtPartNo.Text = String.Empty
                    Return
                End If
                txtPartDescEngOp.Text = t.Rows(0).Item("partdesc")

                Selected_PartNo = txtPartNo.Text
                Selected_PartDesc = txtPartDescEngOp.Text

                If _selected_ifs_po Is Nothing And (cboRef4.SelectedIndex = 0 Or cboRef4.SelectedValue = "MANFADD" Or cboRef4.SelectedValue = "PURCADD") Then
                    Select Case t.Rows(0).Item("lead_source")
                        Case "M"
                            cboRef4.SelectedValue = "MANFADD"
                            txtPONumber.Text = String.Empty
                        Case "P"
                            cboRef4.SelectedValue = "PURCADD"
                    End Select
                End If
            End Using
        End If
        FilterInventoryInStock()
        RefreshCurrentInventory()
        HandlePartImageGUI()
    End Sub
    Private IsGridLoading As Boolean = False

    Private Async Function GetAllIFSPOsData() As Task
        Dim text = chkRecievedOnly.Text
        Try
            Me.Invoke(Sub()
                          chkRecievedOnly.Enabled = False
                          chkRecievedOnly.Text = "Loading Unreceived data..."
                      End Sub)

            Dim result = Await IFSDAL.GetPOLinesQuickReport()

            If result IsNot Nothing Then
                Me.Invoke(Sub()
                              _ifs_data = result
                          End Sub)
            End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            Me.Invoke(Sub()
                          Console.WriteLine($"PO Data Loading Error: {ex.Message}")
                          Dim toast As New ToastNotification("Error loading the rest of the PO Data!", Color.Red)
                          toast.Show()
                      End Sub)
        Finally
            Me.Invoke(Sub()
                          chkRecievedOnly.Enabled = True
                          chkRecievedOnly.Text = text
                      End Sub)
        End Try
    End Function


    Private Async Function FillERPGrid() As Task
        If (IsGridLoading) Then
            Return
        End If
        Try
            IsGridLoading = True
            With ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.DisplayLayout.Override
                .AllowUpdate = DefaultableBoolean.False
                .CellClickAction = CellClickAction.RowSelect
                .SelectTypeRow = SelectType.Single
                .SelectTypeCell = SelectType.None
                .RowSelectors = DefaultableBoolean.True
            End With

            Me.Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor
            Dim result = Await IFSDAL.GetReceivedPOLinesQuickReport(Nothing)
            Me.Cursor = Cursors.Default

            If (result IsNot Nothing) Then
                lblPoExistance.Visible = result.Count <= 0

                _ifs_data = result
                Dim data = GetErpBaseData()

                ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.DataSource = data.ToList()
                ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.Selected.Rows.Clear()
                ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow = Nothing

                ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.Rows.ExpandAll(True)
                With ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.DisplayLayout.Bands(0)
                    .Columns("FinesseDescription").Width = 200
                    .Columns("SupplierName").Width = 200
                    .Columns("DateEntered").Width = 70
                End With

                ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.DisplayLayout.Bands(1).Columns("ChildPartDescription").Width = 200

                filter_ERP_DataGrid(txtERPPoNumber.Text)
            End If
            If (Not {UserAccess.RECIEVED_PO_MANDATORY, UserAccess.NO_ACCESS, UserAccess.GENERAL_USER}.Contains(UserPermission)) Then
                Dim unused = Task.Run(Async Function()
                                          Await GetAllIFSPOsData() 'This is way much slower than GetReceivedPOLinesQuickReport, loading first the received only to show something and load the rest of the data on the background.
                                          'Me.Invoke(New Action(Sub()
                                          '                         filter_ERP_DataGrid(txtERPPoNumber.Text)
                                          '                     End Sub))
                                      End Function)
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            Me.Cursor = Cursors.Default
            Console.WriteLine("Error loading PO Data")
            MsgBox(ex.Message, MsgBoxStyle.Critical)
        Finally
            IsGridLoading = False
            Me.Cursor = Cursors.Default
        End Try


    End Function

    Private Sub ReloadSavedPOs(refreshedData As DataTable)
        Try

            Me.Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor
            If (refreshedData IsNot Nothing AndAlso refreshedData.Rows.Count > 0) Then
                For Each item As DataRow In refreshedData.Rows
                    Dim newQty
                    Dim dataResult As DataTable
                    Using conn = GetOpenedFinesseConnection()
                        Dim cmd = conn.CreateCommand
                        cmd.CommandText = "SELECT TOP(1) OrderNo, PartNumber, Qty, Line_No, Release_No 
                                           FROM IFS.InventoryReceipts
                                           WHERE OrderNo = @OrderNo
                                             AND PartNumber = @PartNumber
                                             AND Line_No = @LineNo
                                             AND Release_No = @ReleaseNo"

                        cmd.Parameters.Clear()
                        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@OrderNo", item("PONumber"))
                        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@PartNumber", item("partno"))
                        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@LineNo", item("POLineNo"))
                        cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("@ReleaseNo", item("POReleaseNo"))
                        Using reader = cmd.ExecuteReader()
                            If reader.Read() Then
                                If Not reader.IsDBNull(reader.GetOrdinal("Qty")) Then
                                    newQty = Integer.Parse(reader("Qty"))
                                End If
                            End If
                        End Using
                    End Using

                    Dim element = _ifs_data.FirstOrDefault(Function(po) po.PartNo = item("partno") AndAlso po.OrderNo = item("PONumber") AndAlso po.LineNo = item("POLineNo") AndAlso po.ReleaseNo = item("POReleaseNo"))
                    If (element IsNot Nothing AndAlso newQty IsNot Nothing) Then
                        element.FinessePart.QtyInFinesse = newQty
                    End If
                Next
                Dim selectedRow = GetParentchildSelectedRow()

                ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.DataSource = _ifs_data.ToList()
                ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.Selected.Rows.Clear()
                ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow = Nothing

                filter_ERP_DataGrid(txtERPPoNumber.Text)
                SelectPreviousSelectionOnGrid(selectedRow.Item1, selectedRow.Item2)
            End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            Me.Cursor = Cursors.Default
            Console.WriteLine(ex.Message)
            Dim toast As New ToastNotification("Error loading saved PO Data to the grid", Color.Red)
            toast.Show()
        Finally
            Me.Cursor = Cursors.Default
        End Try
    End Sub


    Private clickedRow As UltraGridRow = Nothing




    Private Sub ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders_MouseDown(sender As Object, e As MouseEventArgs) Handles ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.MouseDown
        If e.Button = MouseButtons.Right Then
            Dim uiElement = ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.DisplayLayout.UIElement.ElementFromPoint(New Point(e.X, e.Y))
            Dim row = TryCast(uiElement.GetContext(GetType(UltraGridRow)), UltraGridRow)


            If row IsNot Nothing Then
                clickedRow = row

                Dim po = TryCast(clickedRow.ListObject, QuickReportSchema)

                BtnAddSubparts.Visible = _can_add_edit_disassemblies_subparts
                UltraGridMenuStrip.Show(ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders, New Point(e.X, e.Y))
                menuItemSendMessage.Visible = _can_add_edit_disassemblies_subparts AndAlso po IsNot Nothing AndAlso po.IsRecieved
                Dim qty = If(po.QtyOnHand < po.InvQtyArrived, po.QtyOnHand, po.InvQtyArrived)
                txtQuantityMessage.Text = qty
            End If
        End If
    End Sub


    Private Sub CopyPONumberToolStripMenuItem_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles CopyPONumberToolStripMenuItem.Click, CopyPartNumberToolStripMenuItem.Click
        Dim clickedItem = CType(sender, ToolStripMenuItem)

        Dim partno As String
        Dim ponumber As String


        If (clickedRow.HasParent) Then
            Dim row = TryCast(clickedRow.ListObject, DisassemblyPart)
            partno = row.ChildPartNo
            ponumber = row.OrderNo
        Else
            Dim row = TryCast(clickedRow.ListObject, QuickReportSchema)
            partno = row.PartNo
            ponumber = row.OrderNo
        End If

        Dim copiedText As String
        If clickedItem Is CopyPONumberToolStripMenuItem Then
            copiedText = ponumber
        ElseIf clickedItem Is CopyPartNumberToolStripMenuItem Then
            copiedText = partno
        End If
        Clipboard.SetText(copiedText)

        Dim toast As New ToastNotification($"{copiedText} Copied to clipboard!", Color.Green)
        toast.Show()
    End Sub

    Private Sub ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders_InitializeRow(sender As Object, e As Infragistics.Win.UltraWinGrid.InitializeRowEventArgs) Handles ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.InitializeRow

        Dim row = e.Row
        row.Selected = False
        If row.Band.Index = 0 Then
            Dim PoLine = TryCast(row.ListObject, QuickReportSchema)
            If (PoLine.FinessePart Is Nothing) Then
                If (PoLine.Disassemblies Is Nothing OrElse PoLine.Disassemblies.Count = 0) Then
                    row.ToolTipText = "This part does not exist in Finesse, and no subparts have been added. Please create the part or add the required subparts."
                End If
                row.Appearance.BackColor = ColorPalette.DangerRow
            End If

            If (row.HasChild) Then
                row.ToolTipText = "This PO Line contains Subparts. Please receive all the subparts."
                row.Appearance.BackColor = ColorPalette.GrayRow
            End If
        End If

    End Sub


    Private Sub SelectPartOnErpGrid()
        Try
            If (ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders IsNot Nothing AndAlso ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.Rows.Count = 1) Then
                ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow = Nothing
                ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.Rows(0).Activate()
                Return
            End If

            If (_selected_ifs_po IsNot Nothing AndAlso txtPartNo.Text = _selected_ifs_po.PartNo) Then
                Return
            End If


            'Dim erpData = ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.Rows
            'If (erpData IsNot Nothing) Then
            '    Dim rows = erpData.ToList().Where(Function(item) item.Cells("OrderNo").Value.ToString = txtPONumber.Text)
            '    For Each row In rows
            '        row.Activated = (row.Cells("PartNo").Value.ToString = txtPartNo.Text)
            '        If (row.HasChild) Then
            '            For Each childBand In row.ChildBands
            '                For Each childRow In childBand.Rows
            '                    childRow.Activated = (childRow.Cells("ChildPartNo").Value.ToString = txtPartNo.Text)
            '                Next
            '            Next
            '        End If
            '    Next
            'End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            Console.WriteLine("Error activating selected po line: " + ex.Message)
        End Try
    End Sub


    Private Sub ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders_BeforeRowDeactivate(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.BeforeRowDeactivate
        Console.WriteLine("Deactivating selected row...")
        _selected_ifs_po = Nothing
        ReloadPermissions()
    End Sub

    Private Sub ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders_AfterRowActivate(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.AfterRowActivate
        Try
            If (IsGridLoading) Then
                Exit Sub
            End If

            Dim selectedRow As UltraGridRow = ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow
            Dim PoLine As QuickReportSchema
            If (Not selectedRow.HasParent) Then
                PoLine = TryCast(selectedRow.ListObject, QuickReportSchema)
                Dim childBand = selectedRow.ChildBands(0)
                If childBand.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                    childBand.Rows(0).Activate()
                    Exit Sub
                End If
            Else
                Dim disassembly = TryCast(selectedRow.ListObject, DisassemblyPart)
                Dim parent = TryCast(selectedRow.ParentRow.ListObject, QuickReportSchema).Clone()

                If (parent.PartNo <> disassembly.ChildPartNo) Then
                    parent.PartNo = disassembly.ChildPartNo
                    parent.IFSInventory = parent.IFSInventory.Where(Function(item) item.PartNo = disassembly.ChildPartNo).ToList()
                    parent.InvQtyArrived = disassembly.Quantity
                    'parent.FinesseQty = 0
                End If

                If (parent IsNot Nothing AndAlso Not parent.IsSerializedPo) Then
                    txtQty.Text = disassembly.Quantity
                Else
                    txtQty.Text = 1
                End If
                PoLine = parent
            End If
            _selected_ifs_po = PoLine
            If (PoLine IsNot Nothing) Then
                cboRef4.SelectedValue = "PURCADD"
                If (Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(PoLine.Owner)) Then
                    cboOwner.SelectedValue = PoLine.Owner
                End If
                If (Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(PoLine.Warehouse)) Then
                    cboWarehouse.SelectedValue = PoLine.Warehouse
                End If

                txtPONumber.Text = PoLine.OrderNo
                txtPartNo.Text = PoLine.PartNo
                hideOrDisplayAutoIPAddressForPart(PoLine.PartNo)
                ReloadPermissions()
            End If

        Catch ex As Exception
            Console.WriteLine("Error Activating ultragrid row...")
            Console.WriteLine(ex.Message)
        End Try
    End Sub

    Private Function GetErpBaseData() As IEnumerable(Of QuickReportSchema)
        If (_ifs_data IsNot Nothing AndAlso cboErpWarehouse.SelectedItem IsNot Nothing) Then
            Dim selectedCompany = cboErpWarehouse.SelectedItem.Row("IFSCompanyCd").ToString
            Dim result = _ifs_data.Where(Function(po) _
                                        (String.IsNullOrEmpty(selectedCompany) OrElse String.IsNullOrEmpty(po.Contract) OrElse po.Contract = selectedCompany OrElse po.Warehouse = selectedCompany) AndAlso
    (String.IsNullOrEmpty(cboErpOwner.SelectedValue) OrElse String.IsNullOrEmpty(po.Owner) OrElse po.Owner = cboErpOwner.SelectedValue) AndAlso
                                        (chkUnexistingParts.Checked OrElse (Not chkUnexistingParts.Checked AndAlso (po.FinessePart IsNot Nothing OrElse (po.Disassemblies IsNot Nothing AndAlso po.Disassemblies.Count > 0)))) AndAlso
    (Not chkRecievedOnly.Checked OrElse
        (po.IsRecieved = True AndAlso
         CInt(po.FinesseQty) < CInt(po.BuyQtyDue) AndAlso 'Filtering by Qty ordered instead of qty arrived to show rows that haven't been fully receipt.
         (po.FinessePart IsNot Nothing OrElse po.Disassemblies IsNot Nothing)))
)



            Return result
        End If

        Return New List(Of QuickReportSchema)()
    End Function



    Private Sub filter_ERP_DataGrid(Optional search As String = Nothing)
        If (_ifs_data IsNot Nothing) Then
            Dim data = GetErpBaseData()
            Dim dataResult As IEnumerable(Of QuickReportSchema) = data
            If (Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(search)) Then
                Dim lowerSearchText = search.ToLower()

                Dim allWords = lowerSearchText.Split({" "}, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries)

                dataResult = data.Where(Function(po) _
                                        allWords.All(Function(word) _
                                            (po.PartNo IsNot Nothing AndAlso po.PartNo.ToLower().Contains(word)) OrElse
                                            (po.FinesseDescription IsNot Nothing AndAlso po.FinesseDescription.ToLower().Contains(word)) OrElse
                                            (po.OrderNo IsNot Nothing AndAlso po.OrderNo.ToLower().Contains(word)) OrElse
                                            (po.SupplierID IsNot Nothing AndAlso po.SupplierID.ToLower().Contains(word)) OrElse
                                            (po.SupplierName IsNot Nothing AndAlso po.SupplierName.ToLower().Contains(word)) OrElse
                                            (po.PoState IsNot Nothing AndAlso po.PoState.ToLower().Contains(word))
                                        )
                                    )

            End If
            ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.DataSource = (dataResult.ToList())

            ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.Selected.Rows.Clear()
            ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow = Nothing

            ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.Rows.ExpandAll(True)

            SelectPartOnErpGrid()
            ReloadPermissions()
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Sub txtBarcode_TextChanged(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As EventArgs) Handles txtBarcode.TextChanged, txtSerialNo.TextChanged
        If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtSerialNo.Text) Or Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtBarcode.Text) Then
            txtQty.Text = 1
            txtQty.ReadOnly = True
        Else
            txtQty.Clear()
            txtQty.ReadOnly = False
        End If
        If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(Trim(txtBarcode.Text)) Then
            Dim enc As New DmtxImageEncoder
            Dim dmOptions As New DmtxImageEncoderOptions

            dmOptions.SizeIdx = DmtxSymbolSize.DmtxSymbol14x14
            dmOptions.MarginSize = 5
            dmOptions.Scheme = DmtxScheme.DmtxSchemeAscii
            Try
                Dim bcDataMatrix = enc.EncodeImage(txtBarcode.Text, dmOptions)
                pb2Dbarcode.Image = bcDataMatrix
            Catch ex As Exception

            End Try

        Else
            pb2Dbarcode.Image = Nothing
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Sub txtBarcode_Validating(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As System.ComponentModel.CancelEventArgs) Handles txtBarcode.Validating
        If IsFormClosing() = True Then Exit Sub
        If String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtBarcode.Text) Then Exit Sub
        Using newConn As New SqlConnection(FinesseConnectionString)
            newConn.Open()

            Dim exists = newConn.ExecuteScalar("select top 1 unique_no from inpartsub where unique_no = " & SQLQuote(txtBarcode.Text) & " and (onhand > 0 or parentunique_no <> '')")
            If Not exists Is Nothing Then
                Beep_Fail()
                MsgBox("This barcode is already in use.  Please enter a valid barcode.", vbOKOnly)
                txtBarcode.Clear()
                txtBarcode.Focus()
                txtBarcode.SelectAll()
            End If
        End Using
    End Sub

    Private Sub dgvItemsForImport_SelectionChanged(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles dgvItemsForImport.SelectionChanged
        ItemsForImportQtyUpdating()
    End Sub

    Private Sub dgvItemsForImport_CellEndEdit(sender As Object, e As DataGridViewCellEventArgs) Handles dgvItemsForImport.CellEndEdit
        Dim index = 0 'Replacing the usage of this from old clair new usage: PartdescDataGridViewTextBoxColumn.Index
        Select Case e.ColumnIndex
            Case dgcAddItemsQty.Index
                If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemsBarcode.Index, e.RowIndex).Value) And String.IsNullOrEmpty(dgvItemsForImport.Item(e.ColumnIndex, e.RowIndex).Value) <> 1 Then
                    Beep_Fail()
                    MsgBox("Only quantities of 1 can be entered for barcoded items.", vbOKOnly)
                    dgvItemsForImport.Item(e.ColumnIndex, e.RowIndex).Value = 1
                    ItemsForImportQtyUpdating()
                End If
                Dim orderNo = dgvItemsForImport.Item(PONumber.Index, e.RowIndex).Value
                Dim partNumber = dgvItemsForImport.Item(PartnoDataGridViewTextBoxColumn.Index, e.RowIndex).Value
                Dim POLimit = _ifs_data.Where(Function(item) item.OrderNo = orderNo AndAlso item.PartNo = partNumber).FirstOrDefault()
                If String.IsNullOrEmpty(dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemsBarcode.Index, e.RowIndex).Value) AndAlso POLimit IsNot Nothing AndAlso POLimit.UnrecievedQty < dgvItemsForImport.Item(e.ColumnIndex, e.RowIndex).Value Then
                    Beep_Fail()
                    MsgBox("Overstocking inventory is not allowed. The quantity added to the inventory cannot exceed the quantity specified in the Purchase Order.", vbOKOnly)
                    dgvItemsForImport.Item(e.ColumnIndex, e.RowIndex).Value = POLimit.UnrecievedQty
                    ItemsForImportQtyUpdating()
                End If

            Case dgcAddItemsBarcode.Index, dgcAddItemsSerialNo.Index
                If String.IsNullOrEmpty(dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemsBarcode.Index, e.RowIndex).Value) And String.IsNullOrEmpty(dgvItemsForImport.Item(e.ColumnIndex, e.RowIndex).Value) <> 1 Then
                    MsgBox("Only quantities of 1 can be entered for barcoded items.", vbOKOnly)
                End If

            Case dgcAddItemRef4.Index
                If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemsBarcode.Index, e.RowIndex).Value) Then
                    If dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemRef4.Index, e.RowIndex).Value = "NONTOBAR" And dgvItemsForImport.Item(RemoveNonCoded.Index, e.RowIndex).Value = False Then
                        Dim result = MsgBox("You have changed your reason for adding inventory to '" & _dtReasonRef4.Select("ref4='NONTOBAR'")(0)("description") & "'" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & "Would you like to attempt to remove a related non-barcoded item from " & dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemsBld.Index, e.RowIndex).FormattedValue & " INSTOCK (Owner: " & dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemsDept.Index, e.RowIndex).FormattedValue & ")?", vbYesNo)
                        If result = MsgBoxResult.Yes Then
                            dgvItemsForImport.Item(RemoveNonCoded.Index, e.RowIndex).Value = True
                            ValidateAddToInventoryItems()
                        Else
                            MsgBox("Select a different reason for adding inventory.", vbOKOnly)
                        End If
                    End If
                    If Not dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemRef4.Index, e.RowIndex).Value = "NONTOBAR" And dgvItemsForImport.Item(RemoveNonCoded.Index, e.RowIndex).Value = True Then
                        MsgBox("You have changed your reason for adding inventory from '" & _dtReasonRef4.Select("ref4='NONTOBAR'")(0)("description") & "'." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & "The 'Remove Non-Coded' checkbox will be unchecked.", vbOKOnly)
                        dgvItemsForImport.Item(RemoveNonCoded.Index, e.RowIndex).Value = False
                    End If
                End If

            Case dgcAddItemsDept.Index
                If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemsBarcode.Index, e.RowIndex).Value) Then
                    If dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemRef4.Index, e.RowIndex).Value = "NONTOBAR" Then
                        Dim result = MsgBox("You have changed the owner of " & dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemsBarcode.Index, e.RowIndex).Value & ": (" & dgvItemsForImport.Item(PartnoDataGridViewTextBoxColumn.Index, e.RowIndex).Value & ") " & dgvItemsForImport.Item(index, e.RowIndex).Value & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & "Would you like to attempt to remove a related non-barcoded item from " & dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemsBld.Index, e.RowIndex).FormattedValue & " INSTOCK (Owner: " & dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemsDept.Index, e.RowIndex).FormattedValue & ")?", vbYesNo)
                        If result = MsgBoxResult.Yes Then
                            dgvItemsForImport.Item(RemoveNonCoded.Index, e.RowIndex).Value = True
                            ValidateAddToInventoryItems()
                        End If
                    End If
                    If Not dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemRef4.Index, e.RowIndex).Value = "NONTOBAR" And dgvItemsForImport.Item(RemoveNonCoded.Index, e.RowIndex).Value = True Then
                        MsgBox("You have changed the owner of " & dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemsBarcode.Index, e.RowIndex).Value & ": (" & dgvItemsForImport.Item(PartnoDataGridViewTextBoxColumn.Index, e.RowIndex).Value & ") " & dgvItemsForImport.Item(index, e.RowIndex).Value & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & "The 'Remove Non-Coded' checkbox will be unchecked.", vbOKOnly)
                        dgvItemsForImport.Item(RemoveNonCoded.Index, e.RowIndex).Value = False
                    End If
                End If

            Case dgcAddItemsBld.Index
                If Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemsBarcode.Index, e.RowIndex).Value) Then
                    If dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemRef4.Index, e.RowIndex).Value = "NONTOBAR" And dgvItemsForImport.Item(RemoveNonCoded.Index, e.RowIndex).Value = False Then
                        Dim result = MsgBox("You have changed the warehouse of " & dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemsBarcode.Index, e.RowIndex).Value & ": (" & dgvItemsForImport.Item(PartnoDataGridViewTextBoxColumn.Index, e.RowIndex).Value & ") " & dgvItemsForImport.Item(index, e.RowIndex).Value & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & "Would you like to attempt to remove a related non-barcoded item from " & dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemsBld.Index, e.RowIndex).FormattedValue & " INSTOCK (Owner: " & dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemsDept.Index, e.RowIndex).FormattedValue & ")?", vbYesNo)
                        If result = MsgBoxResult.Yes Then
                            dgvItemsForImport.Item(RemoveNonCoded.Index, e.RowIndex).Value = True
                            ValidateAddToInventoryItems()
                        End If
                    End If
                    If Not dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemRef4.Index, e.RowIndex).Value = "NONTOBAR" And dgvItemsForImport.Item(RemoveNonCoded.Index, e.RowIndex).Value = True Then
                        MsgBox("You have changed the warehouse of " & dgvItemsForImport.Item(dgcAddItemsBarcode.Index, e.RowIndex).Value & ": (" & dgvItemsForImport.Item(PartnoDataGridViewTextBoxColumn.Index, e.RowIndex).Value & ") " & dgvItemsForImport.Item(index, e.RowIndex).Value & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & "The 'Remove Non-Coded' checkbox will be unchecked.", vbOKOnly)
                        dgvItemsForImport.Item(RemoveNonCoded.Index, e.RowIndex).Value = False
                    End If
                End If
        End Select
    End Sub

    Private Sub txtSerialNo_Validating(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As System.ComponentModel.CancelEventArgs) Handles txtSerialNo.Validating
        If IsFormClosing() = True Then Exit Sub
        If String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtSerialNo.Text) Then Exit Sub
        Using newConn As New SqlConnection(FinesseConnectionString)
            newConn.Open()
            Dim exists = newConn.ExecuteScalar("select top 1 serial_no from inpartsub where serial_no = " & SQLQuote(txtSerialNo.Text) & " and partno = " & SQLQuote(txtPartNo.Text) & " and (onhand > 0 or parentunique_no <> '')")
            If Not exists Is Nothing Then
                Beep_Fail()
                MsgBox("This serial number is already in use for this part.  Please enter a valid serial number.", vbOKOnly)
                txtSerialNo.Clear()
                txtSerialNo.Focus()
                txtSerialNo.SelectAll()
            End If
        End Using
    End Sub

#Region "Utilities"

    Private Sub GotPartsAsync(ByVal result As IAsyncResult)
        Dim cmd As SqlCommand = CType(result.AsyncState, SqlCommand)
        Dim reader As SqlDataReader = cmd.EndExecuteReader(result)
        Invoke(
            Sub()
                Try
                    If cmd Is _expectedCommand Then
                        _dtClairNewSerialNos.Load(reader)

                        'If (_dtClairNewSerialNos.Rows.Count > 0) Then
                        '    lblEnterClairNewSerialNo.Visible = False
                        'Else
                        '    lblEnterClairNewSerialNo.Visible = True
                        'End If
                    End If

                Catch ex As Exception
                    Beep_Fail()
                    MsgBox("WTF?" & ex.ToString, MsgBoxStyle.Critical)
                Finally
                    If Not reader Is Nothing Then
                        reader.Close()
                    End If
                    cmd.Connection.Close()
                    cmd.Connection.Dispose()
                    cmd.Dispose()
                End Try
            End Sub)
    End Sub

    Public Sub Beep_Fail()
        My.Computer.Audio.Play(GetEnvironmentVariable("ESSVBDir") + "\sounds\bad_beep.wav", AudioPlayMode.Background)
    End Sub

    Public Sub Beep_Success()
        My.Computer.Audio.Play(GetEnvironmentVariable("ESSVBDir") + "\sounds\good_beep.wav", AudioPlayMode.Background)
    End Sub

#End Region

    Function ValidateAddToInventoryItems()
        Dim dt = DataSetAddInventory.inpartsub
        Dim NBCSummary = From r As DataSetAddInventory.inpartsubRow In dt.Rows
                         Where r.RemoveNonCoded = True
                         Group r By partno = r.partno, partdesc = r.partdesc, owner = r.dept, wh = r.bld Into Group
                         Select partno, partdesc, owner, wh, totalonhand = Group.Sum(Function(r) r.onhand), count = Group.Count()
        Dim dtNBCSummary = NBCSummary.ToDataTable

        For Each dr As DataRow In dtNBCSummary.Rows
            Dim sSQL As New StringBuilder
            sSQL.AppendLine("select onhand from dbo.inpartsub ps where ps.partno = " & SQLQuote(dr.Item("partno")) & " and ps.dept = " & SQLQuote(dr.Item("owner")) & " and ps.bld = " & SQLQuote(dr.Item("wh")) & "  and ps.batchno = 'INSTOCK' and ps.unique_no = '' and ps.parentunique_no = '' and onhand > 0")
            Dim qtyNBCtoRemove = gConn.GetDataTable(sSQL)
            If qtyNBCtoRemove.Rows.Count = 0 OrElse qtyNBCtoRemove.Rows(0).Item("onhand") < dr.Item("totalonhand") Then
                Beep_Fail()
                MsgBox("You have intended to remove non-barcoded inventory when adding barcodes for " & dr.Item("partno") & ": " & dr.Item("partdesc") & ", but " & IIf(qtyNBCtoRemove.Rows.Count = 0 OrElse qtyNBCtoRemove.Rows(0).Item("onhand") = 0, "no", "not enough") & " non-barcoded items exist in INSTOCK (" & dr.Item("wh") & "), owned by " & dr.Item("owner") & "." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & "Try to find a matching non-barcoded item in MIA or on Late Returns to check into your INSTOCK before adding this item, or uncheck the 'Remove Non-Coded' checkbox for this item to INCREASE inventory levels." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & "Commit has been aborted.", vbOKOnly)
                Return False
            End If
        Next
        Return True
    End Function

    Function ItemsForImportQtyUpdating()
        Dim totalItemsAddedQty As Integer = 0
        Dim selectedItemsAddedQty As Integer = 0

        If dgvItemsForImport.Rows.Count <> 0 Then
            Dim t = From r As DataGridViewRow In dgvItemsForImport.Rows
                    Select total = r.DataBoundItem("onhand")
            totalItemsAddedQty = t.Sum(Function(r) r)

            Dim s = From r As DataGridViewRow In dgvItemsForImport.SelectedRows
                    Select total = r.DataBoundItem("onhand")
            selectedItemsAddedQty = s.Sum(Function(r) r)
        End If

        tsslItemsForImportTotalQty.Text = $"Total Qty: {totalItemsAddedQty}"
        tsslItemsForImportSelectedRowQty.Text = $"Selected Qty: {selectedItemsAddedQty}"
        Return True
    End Function

    Private Sub cboOwner_SelectedValueChanged(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles cboOwner.SelectedValueChanged, cboWarehouse.SelectedValueChanged
        RefreshCurrentInventory()
    End Sub

    Private Sub dgvCurrentInventory_CellFormatting(sender As Object, e As DataGridViewCellFormattingEventArgs) Handles dgvCurrentInventory.CellFormatting
        If e.ColumnIndex = dgvCurrentInventory.Columns("Return").Index Then
            If Not IsDBNull(e.Value) AndAlso CDate(e.Value) < Today Then
                dgvCurrentInventory.Rows(e.RowIndex).DefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = Color.Red
            Else
                dgvCurrentInventory.Rows(e.RowIndex).DefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = SystemColors.ControlText
            End If
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Sub btnGetNextBarcode_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles btnGetNextBarcode.Click
        If txtBarcode.Text <> "" Then
            Dim result = MsgBox("Would you like to clear the barcode and retrieve another?", MsgBoxStyle.OkCancel, "Clear barcode?")
            If result = MsgBoxResult.Cancel Then
                Exit Sub
            End If
        End If

        Dim owner = _dtOwners.Select($"OwnerCode = '{cboOwner.SelectedValue}'").FirstOrDefault

        Dim BarcodeSequenceType = owner("BarcodeSequenceType").ToString

        Using newConn As New SqlConnection(FinesseConnectionString)
            newConn.Open()

            Dim conn As SqlConnection = GetOpenedFinesseConnection()
            Dim getSequenceCmd As SqlCommand = New SqlCommand("create_barcode", conn)
            getSequenceCmd.CommandType = CommandType.StoredProcedure
            getSequenceCmd.Parameters.Add("@SequenceType", SqlDbType.VarChar, 255).Value = BarcodeSequenceType
            getSequenceCmd.Parameters.Add("@nextBarcode", SqlDbType.VarChar, 50)
            getSequenceCmd.Parameters("@nextBarcode").Direction = ParameterDirection.Output
            getSequenceCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
            txtBarcode.Text = getSequenceCmd.Parameters.Item("@nextBarcode").Value.ToString
        End Using
    End Sub

    Private Function EnabledZebraPrinting(ownerCode As String) As Boolean
        Dim selectedOwner = _dtOwners.Select($"OwnerCode = {ownerCode.SQLQuote}")?.FirstOrDefault()
        If (selectedOwner IsNot Nothing And Not IsDBNull(selectedOwner("EnableZebraPrinting"))) Then
            Dim EnableZebraPrinting As Boolean = Convert.ToBoolean(selectedOwner("EnableZebraPrinting"))
            Return EnableZebraPrinting
        End If
        Return False
    End Function

    Private Sub UpdatePrintBehavior()
        Dim activateZebraPrint As Boolean = EnabledZebraPrinting(cboOwner.SelectedValue)

        _useZebraPrinting = activateZebraPrint
        pb2Dbarcode.Visible = Not activateZebraPrint
        btnGetNextBarcode.Visible = activateZebraPrint
        tsmPrintSelectedItemsForImport.Visible = activateZebraPrint
        PrintToolStripMenuItem.Visible = Not activateZebraPrint
        PrintToolStripMenuItem.Enabled = Not activateZebraPrint
        PrintPreviewToolStripMenuItem.Visible = Not activateZebraPrint

        tsmPrintBarcodes.Visible = activateZebraPrint
        tsmPrintBarcodes.Enabled = activateZebraPrint
        PrintBArcodesSelectedItemsToolStripMenuItem.Enabled = activateZebraPrint
        PrintBArcodesSelectedItemsToolStripMenuItem.Visible = activateZebraPrint

        If activateZebraPrint Then
            PrintToolStripMenuItem.ShortcutKeys = Nothing
            PrintPreviewToolStripMenuItem.ShortcutKeys = Nothing
            tsmPrintBarcodes.ShortcutKeys = Shortcut.CtrlP
            PrintBArcodesSelectedItemsToolStripMenuItem.ShortcutKeys = Shortcut.CtrlShiftP
        Else
            tsmPrintBarcodes.ShortcutKeys = Nothing
            PrintBArcodesSelectedItemsToolStripMenuItem.ShortcutKeys = Nothing
            PrintToolStripMenuItem.ShortcutKeys = Shortcut.CtrlP
            PrintPreviewToolStripMenuItem.ShortcutKeys = Shortcut.CtrlShiftP

        End If

    End Sub

    Private Sub cboOwner_SelectionChangeCommitted(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles cboOwner.SelectionChangeCommitted
        UpdatePrintBehavior()
    End Sub

    Private Sub txtMACAddress_TextChanged(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles txtMACAddress.TextChanged
        If IsNothing(_searchTimer) Then
            _searchTimer = New Threading.Timer(AddressOf invokeMACAddressValidation, Nothing, _searchDelay, Timeout.Infinite)
        Else
            _searchTimer.Change(_searchDelay, Timeout.Infinite)
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Sub invokeMACAddressValidation(ByVal x As Object)
        Dim mi = New MethodInvoker(AddressOf MACAddressValidationSurrogateFunction)
        Invoke(mi)
    End Sub

    Private Sub MACAddressValidationSurrogateFunction()

        If txtMACAddress.Text = String.Empty Then
            Exit Sub
        End If


        'Dim hasLowerCaseLetters = Regex.Match(txtMACAddress.Text, "[a-f]")

        'If hasLowerCaseLetters.Success Or txtMACAddress.Text.Length <> 17 Then
        '    attemptParseMACAddress()
        '    Exit Sub
        'End If


        If Not isValidMACAddress(txtMACAddress.Text) Then

            Dim parsedMACAddress As String = attemptParseMACAddress(txtMACAddress.Text)

            If Not isValidMACAddress(parsedMACAddress) Then
                Beep_Fail()
                MessageBox.Show("This is an invalid MAC address format. Please use the following format 12:34:56:78:9A:BC")
            Else
                txtMACAddress.Text = parsedMACAddress
            End If

        End If

    End Sub

    Private Function isValidMACAddress(MACAddress As String) As Boolean
        Return Regex.IsMatch(MACAddress, "^[0-9A-F]{2}:[0-9A-F]{2}:[0-9A-F]{2}:[0-9A-F]{2}:[0-9A-F]{2}:[0-9A-F]{2}$")
    End Function

    Private Sub DgvInventoryInStock_SelectionChanged(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles DgvInventoryInStock.SelectionChanged
        If (DgvInventoryInStock.SelectedRows.Count = 0) Then
            Exit Sub
        End If

        Dim selected As DataGridViewRow = DgvInventoryInStock.SelectedRows(0)

        If selected.Cells("SerialNo").Value IsNot Nothing Then
            If (selected.Cells("SerialNo").Value.ToString() <> "*") Then
                txtSerialNo.Text = selected.Cells("SerialNo").Value.ToString()
            Else
                txtSerialNo.Text = String.Empty
            End If
            'txtQty.Text = selected.Cells("Qty").Value.ToString()
            'Else
            '    txtSerialNo.Text = String.Empty
        End If
        txtSerialNo.Text = String.Empty
    End Sub
    Private Sub chkDeferEnteringMacAddress_CheckedChanged(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles chkDeferEnteringMacAddress.CheckedChanged

        If chkDeferEnteringMacAddress.Checked Then
            txtMACAddress.Clear()
            txtMACAddress.Enabled = False
            txtSerialNo.Focus()
        Else
            txtMACAddress.Clear()
            txtMACAddress.Enabled = True
            txtMACAddress.Focus()
        End If


    End Sub

    Private Function attemptParseMACAddress(MACAddress As String) As String
        Dim parsedMacAddress = txtMACAddress.Text.ToUpper

        'get rid of all separators
        parsedMacAddress = parsedMacAddress.Replace(".", "")
        parsedMacAddress = parsedMacAddress.Replace("-", "")
        parsedMacAddress = parsedMacAddress.Replace(":", "")
        parsedMacAddress = parsedMacAddress.Replace(" ", "")

        'if not 12 hexidecimal characters without separators then we cannot parse
        If parsedMacAddress.Length = 12 Then
            Dim i = parsedMacAddress.Length - 1
            While i > 0

                If i Mod 2 = 0 AndAlso i <> 0 Then
                    parsedMacAddress = parsedMacAddress.Insert(i, ":")
                End If

                i -= 1
            End While


        Else
            Return parsedMacAddress
        End If

        Return parsedMacAddress
    End Function

    Private Sub tabControl1_SelectedIndexChanged(ByVal sender As Object, ByVal e As System.EventArgs) Handles TabControl1.SelectedIndexChanged
        If TabControl1.SelectedTab?.Name = ImagesTab.Name Then
            InitPartImages()
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Sub InitPartImages()
        Try
            If TabControl1.SelectedTab?.Name = ImagesTab.Name Then
                If (_FileExplorer Is Nothing) Then
                    InitPartFileExplorer()
                End If
                If (Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(Selected_PartNo) Or Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtPartNo.Text)) Then
                    ImagesTab.Enabled = True
                    LblNonPartsImages.SendToBack()
                Else
                    ImagesTab.Enabled = False
                    LblNonPartsImages.BringToFront()
                End If
                ImagesTab.Controls.Clear()
                _PartImages = New PartImagesPanel(_FileExplorer)
                ImagesTab.Controls.Add(_PartImages)
                _PartImages.Dock = DockStyle.Fill
                _PartImages.Visible = True
                _PartImages.InitTreeView()
            End If
        Catch ex As Exception

        End Try
    End Sub


    Private Sub InitPartFileExplorer()
        If (_FileExplorer Is Nothing) Then
            _FileExplorer = New FileExplorer("Parts")
        End If
        Dim newGUID = getPartGUID(If(String.IsNullOrEmpty(Selected_PartNo), txtPartNo.Text, Selected_PartNo))
        If ((String.IsNullOrEmpty(_FileExplorer.currentGUID) Or Selected_Barcode <> newGUID) And Not String.IsNullOrEmpty(newGUID)) Then
            Selected_GUID = newGUID
            _FileExplorer.rootFolderChanged(Selected_GUID)
            InitPartImages()
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Function getPartGUID(partno As String) As String
        If (String.IsNullOrEmpty(partno)) Then
            Return ""
        End If

        Dim partGUID As String = String.Empty
        Try
            Dim jammer As New SQLJammer(GetOpenedFinesseConnection())
            jammer.Add($"
                SELECT TOP 1 GUID FROM dbo.inpart
                WHERE partno =  {SQLQuote(partno)}",
                     Sub(result)
                         If result.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                             partGUID = result.Rows(0).Item("GUID").ToString()
                         End If
                     End Sub)
            jammer.Execute()
        Catch ex As Exception
            MessageBox.Show("Error Getting File Storage GUID: " & ex.Message)
        End Try
        Return partGUID
    End Function


    Private Sub FilterInventoryInStock()
        If (_selected_ifs_po Is Nothing OrElse String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtPartNo.Text) OrElse String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtPONumber.Text) OrElse cboRef4.SelectedValue <> "PURCADD") Then
            Exit Sub
        End If

        Dim result = _selected_ifs_po.IFSInventory.Where(Function(item) item.AvailableQty > 0)
        DgvInventoryInStock.DataSource = result.ToList()


    End Sub

    End Sub

    Private Async Sub btnReloadIFS_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles btnReloadIFS.Click
        Dim selectedValues = GetParentchildSelectedRow()

        Await FillERPGrid()
        filter_ERP_DataGrid(txtERPPoNumber.Text)
        SelectPreviousSelectionOnGrid(selectedValues.Item1, selectedValues.Item2)
    End Sub

    Private Function GetParentchildSelectedRow() As (QuickReportSchema, DisassemblyPart)
        Dim Selected = ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow
        If (Selected Is Nothing) Then
            Return (Nothing, Nothing)
        End If
        Dim IsChild = Selected.HasParent
        Dim parent
        Dim child
        If (IsChild) Then
            parent = TryCast(Selected.ParentRow.ListObject, QuickReportSchema)
            child = TryCast(Selected.ListObject, DisassemblyPart)
        Else
            parent = TryCast(Selected.ListObject, QuickReportSchema)
        End If

        Return (parent, child)
    End Function

    Private Sub SelectPreviousSelectionOnGrid(parent As QuickReportSchema, child As DisassemblyPart)
        If (parent IsNot Nothing) Then
            Dim IsChild = child IsNot Nothing
            Dim row = ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.Rows.FirstOrDefault(Function(item) item.Cells("OrderNo").Value = parent.OrderNo AndAlso item.Cells("PartNo").Value = parent.PartNo)
            If (row IsNot Nothing) Then
                If (IsChild) Then
                    Dim childRow = row.ChildBands(0).Rows.FirstOrDefault(Function(item) item.Cells("ChildPartNo").Value = child.ChildPartNo)
                    If (childRow IsNot Nothing) Then
                        childRow.Activate()
                    End If
                End If
                row.Activate()
            End If
        End If
    End Sub

    Private Sub ErpData_SelectedValueChanged(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles cboErpWarehouse.SelectedValueChanged, cboErpOwner.SelectedValueChanged, txtERPPoNumber.TextChanged, chkRecievedOnly.CheckedChanged, chkUnexistingParts.CheckedChanged
        filter_ERP_DataGrid(txtERPPoNumber.Text)
    End Sub

        GetPOMessages()
    End Sub
#End Region

#Region "IFS Messages Tab"
    Private Async Sub GetPOMessages()
        Try

            lblIncomingMsgAlert.Visible = String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtPONumber.Text)
            If (String.IsNullOrEmpty(txtPONumber.Text)) Then
                Exit Sub
            End If

            Dim data = Await IFSDAL.GetIFSInventoryReceiptMessages(txtPONumber.Text)
            lblIncomingMsgAlert.Visible = data.Count = 0
            dgvIFSMessages.DataSource = data.ToList()
            dgvIFSMessages.Columns("ErrorMessage").Width = 700

        Catch ex As Exception
            Console.WriteLine("Error loading Messages Data")
        End Try
    End Sub



    Private Sub BtnRefreshIfsMessages_Click(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles BtnRefreshIfsMessages.Click
        GetPOMessages()
    End Sub

Part: FillERPGrid (PRT-034)
Type: form

Private Async Function FillERPGrid() As Task
        If (IsGridLoading) Then
            Return
        End If
        Try
            IsGridLoading = True
            With ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.DisplayLayout.Override
                .AllowUpdate = DefaultableBoolean.False
                .CellClickAction = CellClickAction.RowSelect
                .SelectTypeRow = SelectType.Single
                .SelectTypeCell = SelectType.None
                .RowSelectors = DefaultableBoolean.True
            End With

            Me.Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor
            Dim result = Await IFSDAL.GetReceivedPOLinesQuickReport(Nothing)
            Me.Cursor = Cursors.Default

            If (result IsNot Nothing) Then
                lblPoExistance.Visible = result.Count <= 0

                _ifs_data = result
                Dim data = GetErpBaseData()

                ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.DataSource = data.ToList()
                ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.Selected.Rows.Clear()
                ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.ActiveRow = Nothing

                ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.Rows.ExpandAll(True)
                With ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.DisplayLayout.Bands(0)
                    .Columns("FinesseDescription").Width = 200
                    .Columns("SupplierName").Width = 200
                    .Columns("DateEntered").Width = 70
                End With

                ultraGridERPPurchaseOrders.DisplayLayout.Bands(1).Columns("ChildPartDescription").Width = 200

                filter_ERP_DataGrid(txtERPPoNumber.Text)
            End If
            If (Not {UserAccess.RECIEVED_PO_MANDATORY, UserAccess.NO_ACCESS, UserAccess.GENERAL_USER}.Contains(UserPermission)) Then
                Dim unused = Task.Run(Async Function()
                                          Await GetAllIFSPOsData() 'This is way much slower than GetReceivedPOLinesQuickReport, loading first the received only to show something and load the rest of the data on the background.
                                          'Me.Invoke(New Action(Sub()
                                          '                         filter_ERP_DataGrid(txtERPPoNumber.Text)
                                          '                     End Sub))
                                      End Function)
            End If


        Catch ex As Exception
            Me.Cursor = Cursors.Default
            Console.WriteLine("Error loading PO Data")
            MsgBox(ex.Message, MsgBoxStyle.Critical)
        Finally
            IsGridLoading = False
            Me.Cursor = Cursors.Default
        End Try


    End Function

        any.Namespace = ds.Namespace

        type.Particle = sequence
        Dim dsSchema As Global.System.Xml.Schema.XmlSchema = ds.GetSchemaSerializable
        If xs.Contains(dsSchema.TargetNamespace) Then
            Dim s1 As Global.System.IO.MemoryStream = New Global.System.IO.MemoryStream()
            Dim s2 As Global.System.IO.MemoryStream = New Global.System.IO.MemoryStream()
            Try 
                Dim schema As Global.System.Xml.Schema.XmlSchema = Nothing
                dsSchema.Write(s1)
                Dim schemas As Global.System.Collections.IEnumerator = xs.Schemas(dsSchema.TargetNamespace).GetEnumerator
                Do While schemas.MoveNext
                    schema = CType(schemas.Current,Global.System.Xml.Schema.XmlSchema)
                    s2.SetLength(0)
                    schema.Write(s2)
                    If (s1.Length = s2.Length) Then
                        s1.Position = 0
                        s2.Position = 0
                        
                        Do While ((s1.Position <> s1.Length)  _
                                    AndAlso (s1.ReadByte = s2.ReadByte))
                            
                            
                        Loop
                        If (s1.Position = s1.Length) Then
                            Return type
                        End If
                    End If
                    
                Loop
            Finally
                If (Not (s1) Is Nothing) Then
                    s1.Close
                End If
                If (Not (s2) Is Nothing) Then
                    s2.Close
                End If
            End Try
        End If
        xs.Add(dsSchema)
        Return type
    End Function

            type.Particle = sequence
            Dim dsSchema As Global.System.Xml.Schema.XmlSchema = ds.GetSchemaSerializable
            If xs.Contains(dsSchema.TargetNamespace) Then
                Dim s1 As Global.System.IO.MemoryStream = New Global.System.IO.MemoryStream()
                Dim s2 As Global.System.IO.MemoryStream = New Global.System.IO.MemoryStream()
                Try 
                    Dim schema As Global.System.Xml.Schema.XmlSchema = Nothing
                    dsSchema.Write(s1)
                    Dim schemas As Global.System.Collections.IEnumerator = xs.Schemas(dsSchema.TargetNamespace).GetEnumerator
                    Do While schemas.MoveNext
                        schema = CType(schemas.Current,Global.System.Xml.Schema.XmlSchema)
                        s2.SetLength(0)
                        schema.Write(s2)
                        If (s1.Length = s2.Length) Then
                            s1.Position = 0
                            s2.Position = 0
                            
                            Do While ((s1.Position <> s1.Length)  _
                                        AndAlso (s1.ReadByte = s2.ReadByte))
                                
                                
                            Loop

                                Return type
                            End If
                        End If
                        
                    Loop

                        s1.Close
                    End If

                    End If
                End Try
